Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DualFact，一个双层多模态事实性评估框架，用于程序性视频字幕生成。该框架将事实正确性分离为概念事实和上下文事实，并通过实验证明其与人类判断的相关性优于标准指标，揭示了现有模型生成的字幕存在系统性遗漏和角色级不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前程序性视频字幕生成的评估方法主要关注流畅性，但往往忽视了事实完整性。现有的最先进多模态语言模型虽然能生成流畅的字幕，但经常在事实层面不完整，存在系统性遗漏和角色级不一致。因此需要一种能够区分抽象语义角色和具体视频实例化的评估框架。

Method: 提出了DualFact双层评估框架：(1)将事实正确性分为概念事实（捕获抽象语义角色：动作、原料、工具、位置）和上下文事实（捕获其在视频中的具体谓词-论元实现）；(2)引入隐式论元增强(VIA)和对比事实集以支持完整和角色一致的评估；(3)实例化为两种模式：DualFact-T（基于文本证据验证）和DualFact-V（基于视频视觉证据验证）。

Result: 在YouCook3-Fact和CraftBench-Fact数据集上的实验表明：(1)最先进的多模态语言模型生成的字幕流畅但事实不完整，存在系统性遗漏和角色级不一致；(2)DualFact与人类事实性判断的相关性显著强于标准指标，特别是在上下文事实方面；(3)仅基于字幕的评估相比视频验证会高估幻觉问题。

Conclusion: DualFact提供了一个可解释且与人类对齐的评估协议，揭示了多模态事实性锚定中的持续挑战。该框架超越了表面流畅性评估，能够有效识别模型生成中的事实遗漏和不一致问题，为程序性视频字幕生成领域提供了更精确的评估方法。

Abstract: We introduce DualFact, a dual-layer, multimodal factuality evaluation framework for procedural video captioning. DualFact separates factual correctness into conceptual facts, capturing abstract semantic roles (e.g., Action, Ingredient, Tool, Location), and contextual facts, capturing their grounded predicate-argument realizations in video. To support complete and role-consistent evaluation, DualFact incorporates implicit argument augmentation (VIA) and contrastive fact sets. We instantiate DualFact in two modes: DualFact-T, which verifies facts against textual evidence, and DualFact-V, which verifies facts against video-grounded visual evidence. Experiments on YouCook3-Fact and CraftBench-Fact show that state-of-the-art multimodal language models produce fluent but often factually incomplete captions, with systematic omissions and role-level inconsistencies. DualFact correlates more strongly with human factuality judgments than standard metrics, particularly for contextual facts, and reveals that caption-only evaluation overestimates hallucinations compared to video-grounded verification. Overall, DualFact offers an interpretable and human-aligned evaluation protocol that highlights persistent challenges in multimodal factual grounding, extending beyond surface-level fluency.

</details>


### [29] [OxyGent: Making Multi-Agent Systems Modular, Observable, and Evolvable via Oxy Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25602)
*Junxing Hu,Tianlong Li,Lei Yu,Ai Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OxyGent，一个开源的多智能体系统（MAS）框架，通过统一的Oxy抽象实现模块化、可观测和可演化，解决了复杂工业环境中MAS部署的可扩展性、可观测性和自主演化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的工业环境中部署生产级多智能体系统（MAS）仍面临重大挑战，主要受限于系统的可扩展性、可观测性以及自主演化能力的不足。

Method: 提出了OxyGent框架，核心包括：1）统一的Oxy抽象，将智能体、工具、LLM和推理流封装为可插拔的原子组件，支持类乐高积木式的组装；2）引入权限驱动的动态规划，以运行时生成的执行图替代僵化工作流，实现自适应可视化；3）集成AI资产管理平台OxyBank，支持自动化数据回流、标注与联合演化。

Result: 实证评估和真实世界的案例研究表明，OxyGent为多智能体系统提供了一个稳健且可扩展的基础架构，有效解决了生产环境部署的关键问题。

Conclusion: OxyGent成功实现了模块化、可观测且可演化的多智能体系统构建，为复杂工业环境下的MAS部署提供了有效的开源解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Deploying production-ready multi-agent systems (MAS) in complex industrial environments remains challenging due to limitations in scalability, observability, and autonomous evolution. We present OxyGent, an open-source framework that enables modular, observable, and evolvable MAS via a unified Oxy abstraction, in which agents, tools, LLMs, and reasoning flows are encapsulated as pluggable atomic components. This Lego-like assembly paradigm supports scalable system composition and non-intrusive monitoring. To enhance observability, OxyGent introduces permission-driven dynamic planning that replaces rigid workflows with execution graphs generated at runtime, which provide adaptive visualizations. To support continuous evolution, the framework integrates OxyBank, an AI asset management platform that supports automated data backflow, annotation, and joint evolution. Empirical evaluations and real-world case studies show that OxyGent provides a robust and scalable foundation for MAS. OxyGent is publicly available at https://oxygent.jd.com/.

</details>


### [30] [The Nonverbal Syntax Framework: An Evidence-Based Tiered System for Inferring Learner States from Observable Behavioral Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25612)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Jaloliddin Rustamov,Zahiriddin Rustamov,Saja Aldabet,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了非言语句法框架，通过对908项研究、17,043个线索-状态映射的系统综述，解决了术语碎片化、证据异质性和状态歧义三大挑战，识别出480个可操作的复制关系（R1-R4），为研究者和从业者提供了证据校准的理论基础和实践工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解学习者的认知和情感状态是自适应教育系统和有效教学的基础。尽管已有研究将非言语线索与内部状态联系起来，但现有研究存在术语描述不一致、证据质量参差不齐、相似行为模式可能指示多种状态等问题，缺乏一个能够将这些线索与证据进行校准的统一框架。

Method: 研究基于908项研究、17,043个线索-状态映射的系统综述。首先通过规范化处理解决了术语碎片化问题，将5,537个状态标签整合为2,010个规范状态（63.7%），将11,521个线索整合为6,434个规范化线索（44.2%）。其次，提出双重证据评估法，分别评估组件证据（线索和状态的覆盖范围）和关系证据（每个线索-状态链接的独立研究数量）。框架包含四个层级：线索词汇表、状态聚类、状态概况和判别分析。

Result: 框架成功整合了九个行为通道的研究成果，识别出480个可操作的R1-R4关系（基于三篇或更多独立论文），覆盖47个关键学习状态和111个不同指标，占映射总数的35.5%。这代表了六十年研究的复制核心。剩余91.5%（9,653个单论文发现）形成了用于复制的探索性假设。研究还发现52%的"极高"关系仅基于一篇论文，证明了分离评估方法的重要性，可避免过度自信的推断。

Conclusion: 非言语句法框架为研究人员提供了识别研究空白的实证基础，为从业者提供了基于证据的状态推断工具，为技术人员提供了多模态检测的有效特征。该框架通过对证据的校准处理，区分了已复制核心与探索性假设，为非言语线索与学习者内部状态的关系研究提供了系统化的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Understanding learners' cognitive and affective states underpins adaptive educational systems and effective teaching. Although research links nonverbal cues to internal states, no framework calibrates them to evidence. We present the Nonverbal Syntax Framework, drawn from a systematic review of 908 studies and 17,043 cue-state mappings (Turaev et al., 2026). The framework addresses three challenges: terminological fragmentation (behaviors described inconsistently), evidence heterogeneity (single observations to replicated findings), and state ambiguity (similar patterns indicating multiple states). Normalization consolidated 5,537 state labels into 2,010 canonical states (63.7%) and 11,521 cues into 6,434 normalized cues (44.2%) across nine behavioral channels. Dual-evidence assessment separately evaluates Component Evidence (coverage of cues and states) and Relationship Evidence (independent studies per cue-state link). 52% of "Very High" relationships rest on one paper, so separation enables calibrated rather than overconfident inference from preliminary findings. The framework's four levels comprise a Cue Vocabulary of 6,434 indicators classified as observable/instrumental; State Clusters linking 2,010 states to indicative cues; State Profiles with multimodal behavioral signatures and actionable specifications; and Discriminative Analysis distinguishing 1,215 confusable state pairs. We identify 480 actionable R1-R4 relationships (three or more independent papers), the replicated core of six decades of research, covering 35.5% of mappings across 47 key learning states and 111 distinct indicators. The remaining 91.5% (9,653 single-paper findings) form exploratory hypotheses for replication. The framework gives researchers an empirical foundation for identifying gaps, practitioners evidence-based tools for state inference, and technologists validated features for multimodal detection.

</details>


### [31] [HotComment: A Benchmark for Evaluating Popularity of Online Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25614)
*Yafeng Wu,Yunyao Zhang,Liliang Ye,Guiyi Zeng,Junqing Yu,Chen Xu,Zikai Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了HotComment多模态基准，用于评估社交媒体评论热度，包含内容质量评估、热度预测和用户行为模拟三个维度；并提出StyleCmt方法，通过风格对齐来增强评论的社会共鸣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评论对社交媒体舆论具有重要影响，但评估其热度仍面临挑战。这不仅需要考虑语言质量、原创性和情感共鸣，还需要应对不同平台和用户群体间风格偏好差异，导致同一评论在不同社区产生不同反响。

Method: 提出了HotComment多模态基准，整合视频和文本模态，从三个维度量化评论热度：（1）内容质量：评估与真实人类评论的语义相似性，并通过四个可解释维度扩展质量评估；（2）热度预测：基于真实交互数据训练的模型趋势；（3）用户行为模拟：通过基于智能体的框架模拟平台用户分布并近似参与度分数。此外，提出StyleCmt方法，受社会涟漪效应启发，通过多风格维度对齐来放大社会共鸣表达并抑制不一致表达。

Result: 构建了一个综合性的多模态评论热度评估基准，能够从内容质量、热度预测和用户行为模拟三个角度全面量化评论的受欢迎程度。StyleCmt方法成功实现了通过风格维度对齐来增强社会共鸣的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究为社交媒体评论热度评估提供了新的多维度框架，通过整合多模态信息和用户行为模拟，能够更准确地预测和解释评论在不同平台上的表现差异，为理解在线评论的社会影响力提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Online comments play a crucial role in shaping public sentiment and opinion dynamics on social media. However, evaluating their popularity remains challenging, not only because it depends on linguistic quality, originality, and emotional resonance, but also because stylistic preferences vary widely across platforms and user groups, causing the same comment to resonate differently in different communities. In this work, we present HotComment, a multimodal benchmark integrating video and text modalities that comprehensively quantifies popularity from three enhanced aspects: (1) Content Quality, which evaluates semantic similarity with ground-truth human comments and extends quality assessment through four interpretable dimensions; (2) Popularity Prediction, based on trends from models trained on real-world interaction data; and (3) User Behavior Simulation, which models the distribution of platform users and approximates \textbf{engagement scores} through an agent-based framework. Furthermore, we propose StyleCmt, inspired by social ripple effects, where multiple stylistic dimensions align to amplify socially resonant expressions and suppress incongruent ones.

</details>


### [32] [Think Before You Act -- A Neurocognitive Governance Model for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25684)
*Eranga Bandara,Ross Gore,Asanga Gunaratna,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Ravi Mukkamala,Sachin Shetty,Xueping Liang,Amin Hass,Tharaka Hewa,Abdul Rahman,Christopher K. Rhea,Anita H. Clayton,Preston Samuel,Atmaram Yarlagadda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经认知治理框架（PAGRL），通过在LLM智能体推理过程中嵌入类似人类自我管理的“行动前治理推理循环”，在企业供应链工作流中实现了95%的合规准确率和零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体在企业和关键领域的快速部署，现有治理方法（如运行时防护栏、训练时对齐和事后审计）将治理视为外部约束而非内化的行为原则，导致智能体容易执行不安全或不可逆的操作，存在根本性的治理缺口。

Method: 受人类自我管理认知过程（执行功能、抑制控制、内化组织规则）启发，构建了行动前治理推理循环（PAGRL）。该方法在智能体执行重要操作前，强制其查询四层治理规则集（全局、工作流特定、智能体特定、情境特定），建立人脑与LLM之间的结构平行，将治理层级嵌入推理核心。

Result: 在生产级零售供应链工作流上的实验表明，该框架实现了95%的合规准确率，且向人类监督层的误报升级次数为零，证明了将治理内嵌于智能体推理比外部强制执行更有效。

Conclusion: 将治理机制嵌入智能体的推理过程能产生比外部强制更一致、可解释和可审计的合规性，为自主AI智能体建立了一个原则性基础，使其能够像人类一样因内化 deliberation（深思熟虑）而进行自我治理，而非被动接受规则。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of autonomous AI agents across enterprise, healthcare, and safety-critical environments has created a fundamental governance gap. Existing approaches, runtime guardrails, training-time alignment, and post-hoc auditing treat governance as an external constraint rather than an internalized behavioral principle, leaving agents vulnerable to unsafe and irreversible actions. We address this gap by drawing on how humans self-govern naturally: before acting, humans engage deliberate cognitive processes grounded in executive function, inhibitory control, and internalized organizational rules to evaluate whether an intended action is permissible, requires modification, or demands escalation. This paper proposes a neurocognitive governance framework that formally maps this human self-governance process to LLM-driven agent reasoning, establishing a structural parallel between the human brain and the large language model as the cognitive core of an agent. We formalize a Pre-Action Governance Reasoning Loop (PAGRL) in which agents consult a four-layer governance rule set: global, workflow-specific, agent-specific, and situational before every consequential action, mirroring how human organizations structure compliance hierarchies across enterprise, department, and role levels. Implemented on a production-grade retail supply chain workflow, the framework achieves 95% compliance accuracy and zero false escalations to human oversight, demonstrating that embedding governance into agent reasoning produces more consistent, explainable, and auditable compliance than external enforcement. This work offers a principled foundation for autonomous AI agents that govern themselves the way humans do: not because rules are imposed upon them, but because deliberation is embedded in how they think.

</details>


### [33] [RADD: Retrieval-Augmented Discrete Diffusion for Multi-Modal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25693)
*Guanglin Niu,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RADD框架，通过解耦检索与重排序来解决多模态知识图谱补全中单一评分器的瓶颈问题，在三个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMKGC模型使用单一嵌入评分器同时进行全局检索和最终决策，但全局高召回率搜索与局部细粒度消歧需要不同的归纳偏置，这种耦合成为核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出检索增强离散扩散（RADD）框架：1）关系感知多模态KGE检索器作为全局检索器和蒸馏教师；2）条件离散去噪器在候选列表级别进行实体身份生成以完成重排序；3）训练结合KGE监督、去噪交叉熵和温度缩放蒸馏；4）推理时Diff-Rerank先用检索器生成Top-K候选列表，再用去噪器重排序。

Result: 在三个MMKGC基准数据集上，RADD优于强力的单模态、多模态和基于LLM的基线方法，消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 解耦检索与重排序能有效解决MMKGC任务中的归纳偏置冲突，确保召回率作为精确率的严格前提，验证了所提框架的有效性和各组件的必要性。

Abstract: Most multi-modal knowledge graph completion (MMKGC) models use one embedding scorer to do both retrieval over the full entity set and final decision making. We argue that this coupling is a core bottleneck: global high-recall search and local fine-grained disambiguation require different inductive biases. Therefore, we propose a Retrieval-Augmented Discrete Diffusion (RADD) framework to decouple retrieve and reranking for MMKGC. A relation-aware multimodal KGE retriever serves as both global retriever and distillation teacher, while a conditional discrete denoiser performs shortlist-level entity-identity generation for reranking. Training combines KGE supervision, denoising cross-entropy, and temperature-scaled distillation from the retriever to the denoiser. At inference, the designed Diff-Rerank first forms a top-$K$ shortlist with the retriever and then reranks it with the denoiser, ensuring that recall is a strict prerequisite for precision. Experiments on three MMKGC benchmarks show that RADD achieves the best performance and consistent gains over strong unimodal, multimodal, and LLM-based baselines, while ablations further verify the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [34] [Scalable Inference Architectures for Compound AI Systems: A Production Deployment Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25724)
*Srikanta Prasad S,Utkarsh Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Salesforce针对复合AI系统（如Agentforce和ApexGuru）设计的模块化推理架构。生产环境数据显示，该架构在P95尾部延迟上降低了50%以上，吞吐量提升高达3.9倍，成本节省30-40%，有效解决了代理工作负载下的多模型扇出开销和冷启动传播等独特挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业AI应用日益依赖由多个模型、检索器和工具组合而成的复合AI系统。现有推理基础设施难以高效支持此类系统的并发、异构模型调用需求，在成本效益和低延迟方面面临挑战，亟需一种能够支撑企业级代理AI规模化运营的新型推理架构。

Method: 提出了一种模块化、平台无关的推理架构，集成了无服务器执行、动态自动伸缩和MLOps管道。该系统旨在支持多组件代理工作流中的并发、异构模型调用，并通过详细案例研究分析了复合系统特有的挑战，包括多模型扇出开销、级联冷启动传播和异构伸缩动态。

Result: 生产环境部署结果表明：P95尾部延迟降低超过50%，吞吐量提升高达3.9倍，相比之前的静态部署实现30-40%的成本节省。架构成功支持了模型调用的并行伸缩、突发性多代理工作负载处理以及快速模型迭代。

Conclusion: 该架构有效解决了复合AI系统在生产部署中的关键挑战，证明了其能够在企业规模上实现代理AI的运营化，为复合AI系统的推理基础设施设计提供了重要的运营经验和参考价值。

Abstract: Modern enterprise AI applications increasingly rely on compound AI systems - architectures that compose multiple models, retrievers, and tools to accomplish complex tasks. Deploying such systems in production demands inference infrastructure that can efficiently serve concurrent, heterogeneous model invocations while maintaining cost-effectiveness and low latency. This paper presents a production deployment study of a modular, platform-agnostic inference architecture developed at Salesforce to support compound AI use cases including Agentforce (autonomous AI agents) and ApexGuru (AI-powered code analysis). The system integrates serverless execution, dynamic autoscaling, and MLOps pipelines to deliver consistent low-latency inference across multi-component agent workflows. We report production results demonstrating over 50% reduction in tail latency (P95), up to 3.9x throughput improvement, and 30 to 40% cost savings compared to prior static deployments. We further present a novel analysis of compound-system-specific challenges including multi-model fan-out overhead, cascading cold-start propagation, and heterogeneous scaling dynamics that emerge uniquely when serving agentic workloads. Through detailed case studies and operational lessons, we illustrate how the architecture enables compound AI systems to scale model invocations in parallel, handle bursty multi-agent workloads, and support rapid model iteration - capabilities essential for operationalizing agentic AI at enterprise scale.

</details>


### [35] [Toward Scalable Terminal Task Synthesis via Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25727)
*Zhiyuan Fan,Tinghao Yu,Yuanjun Cai,Jiangtao Guan,Yun Yang,Dingxin Hu,Jiang Zhou,Xing Wu,Zhuo Han,Feng Zhang,Lilin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SkillSynth框架，通过构建基于场景中介技能图的方法来合成终端任务，显式控制执行轨迹的多样性，解决了现有方法仅关注任务数量扩展而忽视轨迹多样性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端智能体训练受限于高质量、多样化执行轨迹的稀缺性。当前方法虽然通过合成大规模终端任务实例来缓解这一瓶颈，但主要关注任务数量的扩展，对训练期间智能体实际经历的执行轨迹多样性缺乏有效控制。

Method: 提出SkillSynth自动化框架：1）构建大规模技能图，以场景作为连接多样化命令行技能的中间转换节点；2）从图中采样路径作为现实工作流的抽象；3）使用多智能体工具将路径实例化为可执行的任务实例；通过图采样工作流路径显式控制合成任务所需的最小执行轨迹的多样性。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench上的实验证明了SkillSynth的有效性。此外，SkillSynth合成的任务实例已被用于训练Hy3 Preview，增强了其在终端环境下的智能体能力。

Conclusion: SkillSynth成功解决了终端任务合成中执行轨迹多样性控制不足的问题，为终端智能体训练提供了高质量的多样化任务数据，已在实际模型训练中得到验证和应用。

Abstract: Terminal agents have demonstrated strong potential for autonomous command-line execution, yet their training remains constrained by the scarcity of high-quality and diverse execution trajectories. Existing approaches mitigate this bottleneck by synthesizing large-scale terminal task instances for trajectory sampling. However, they primarily focus on scaling the number of tasks while providing limited control over the diversity of execution trajectories that agents actually experience during training. In this paper, we present SkillSynth, an automated framework for terminal task synthesis built on a scenario-mediated skill graph. SkillSynth first constructs a large-scale skill graph, where scenarios serve as intermediate transition nodes that connect diverse command-line skills. It then samples paths from this graph as abstractions of real-world workflows, and uses a multi-agent harness to instantiate them into executable task instances. By grounding task synthesis in graph-sampled workflow paths, SkillSynth explicitly controls the diversity of minimal execution trajectories required to solve the synthesized tasks. Experiments on Terminal-Bench demonstrate the effectiveness of SkillSynth. Moreover, task instances synthesized by SkillSynth have been adopted to train Hy3 Preview, contributing to its enhanced agentic capabilities in terminal-based settings.

</details>


### [36] [QAROO: AI-Driven Online Task Offloading for Energy-Efficient and Sustainable MEC Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25740)
*Yongtao Yao,Yao Yang,Haorui Shi,Canglu Zhu,Miaojiang Chen,Ahmed Farouk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了QAROO框架，一种基于量子神经网络和注意力机制的在线任务卸载方案，解决了传统方法适应性差和收敛慢的问题，在无线供电MEC网络中实现了计算与能源资源的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和智能科学的快速发展，智能边缘计算得到广泛应用。然而传统方法存在适应性差、启发式算法收敛缓慢等局限性，难以满足动态信道环境下可持续、资源高效的边缘应用需求，亟需一种高效稳定的在线任务卸载解决方案。

Method: 提出量子注意力强化学习在线卸载框架(QAROO)，采用二元卸载策略协同优化计算与能源资源。框架集成量子神经网络与注意力机制，引入三项关键改进：使用循环神经网络增强时序建模能力；提出不确定性引导的量化方法提升探索效率；在量子网络中融入注意力机制增强特征表示。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在归一化计算速度和处理时间方面均优于对比方案，验证了其在动态环境中高效稳定的性能表现。

Conclusion: QAROO框架为大规模物联网动态环境中的在线任务卸载提供了高效稳定的解决方案，有效克服了传统方法的局限性，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) and intelligent science, intelligent edge computing has been widely adopted. However, the limitations of traditional methods, such as poor adaptability and the slow convergence of heuristic algorithms, are becoming increasingly evident. To enable sustainable and resource-efficient edge applications, this paper proposes an online task offloading framework for wireless powered mobile edge computing (MEC) networks, called Quantum Attention-based Reinforcement learning for Online Offloading (QAROO). The system employs a binary offloading strategy with the aim of co-optimizing computing and energy resources in dynamic channel environments. In response to the issues of poor adaptability in traditional approaches and the slow convergence of heuristic algorithms, the framework integrates quantum neural networks and attention mechanisms, introducing three key improvements: using recurrent neural networks to enhance temporal modeling capability, proposing an uncertainty-guided quantization method to improve exploration efficiency, and incorporating attention mechanisms into quantum networks to strengthen feature representation. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms comparative schemes in terms of normalized computation speed and processing time, offering an efficient and stable solution for online task offloading in large-scale Internet of Things (IoT) dynamic environments.

</details>


### [37] [StratFormer: Adaptive Opponent Modeling and Exploitation in Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25796)
*Andy Caen,Mark H. M. Winands,Dennis J. N. J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StratFormer，一种基于Transformer的元智能体，通过两阶段课程学习在不完美信息博弈中同时建模和利用对手，在Leduc扑克中实现了平均+0.106 BB/手的剥削收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完美信息博弈中，传统的博弈论最优（GTO）策略虽然安全但无法利用对手的弱点。现有的对手建模方法往往难以在有效利用对手失误和保持策略安全性之间取得平衡。因此需要一种能够同时建模对手行为模式并智能调整剥削程度的方法。

Method: 提出StratFormer架构，采用两阶段课程学习：第一阶段在执行GTO策略的同时训练对手建模头来识别行为模式；第二阶段通过与可利用性相关的逐对手正则化调度，逐步将策略向最佳响应（BR）剥削方向转移。架构创新包括双回合令牌（在智能体和对手决策点构建的特征向量）以及桶率特征（编码对手在五个战略情境下的倾向）。在Leduc扑克上测试，对手包括六种原型和两个强度等级。

Result: 在Leduc Hold'em测试中，面对可利用性从0.15到1.26 BB/手不等的六种对手原型，StratFormer实现了平均+0.106 BB/手的剥削收益，针对高可利用性对手的峰值收益达+0.821 BB/手，同时保持了接近纳什均衡的安全性。

Conclusion: StratFormer成功实现了在不完美信息博弈中安全地建模和剥削对手的目标，证明了基于Transformer的元智能体结合课程学习和专门的架构设计能够有效平衡策略的安全性与剥削性，为复杂博弈中的对手建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present StratFormer, a transformer-based meta-agent that learns to simultaneously model and exploit opponents in imperfect-information games through a two-phase curriculum. The first phase trains an opponent modeling head to identify behavioral patterns from action histories while the agent plays a game-theoretic optimal (GTO) policy. The second phase progressively shifts the policy toward best-response (BR) exploitation, guided by a per-opponent regularization schedule tied to exploitability. Our architecture introduces dual-turn tokens -- feature vectors constructed at both agent and opponent decision points -- coupled with bucket-rate features that encode opponent tendencies across five strategic contexts. On Leduc Hold'em, a small poker variant with six cards and two betting rounds, we test against six opponent archetypes at two strength levels each, with exploitability ranging from 0.15 to 1.26 Big Blinds (BB) per hand. StratFormer achieves an average exploitation gain of +0.106 BB per hand over GTO, with peak gains of +0.821 against highly exploitable opponents, while maintaining near-equilibrium safety.

</details>


### [38] [TrialCalibre: A Fully Automated Causal Engine for RCT Benchmarking and Observational Trial Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25832)
*Amir Habibdoust,Xing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrialCalibre，一个多智能体系统，旨在自动化和扩展BenchExCal框架，以解决真实世界证据（RWE）研究中难以量化的偏倚问题，提高研究的可信度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管真实世界证据（RWE）研究在模拟目标试验方面对监管和临床决策具有重要价值，但残留且难以量化的偏倚仍限制了其可信度。现有的BenchExCal框架虽然方法上有效，但资源消耗大且难以扩展。

Method: 提出了TrialCalibre，一个概念化的多智能体系统，包含Orchestrator、Protocol Design、Data Synthesis、Clinical Validation和Quantitative Calibration等专门智能体，协同完成整体流程。系统集成了智能体学习（如RLHF）和知识黑板，支持自适应、可审计和透明的因果效应估计。

Result: TrialCalibre成功自动化并扩展了BenchExCal工作流程，实现了对因果效应估计的自适应、可审计和透明的处理，提高了研究的效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: TrialCalibre通过多智能体系统的协作与学习机制，有效解决了BenchExCal框架的可扩展性问题，为RWE研究提供了一个自动化、透明且可审计的解决方案，有望提升其在监管和临床决策中的应用价值。

Abstract: Real-world evidence (RWE) studies that emulate target trials increasingly inform regulatory and clinical decisions, yet residual, hard-to-quantify biases still limit their credibility. The recently proposed BenchExCal framework addresses this challenge via a two-stage Benchmark, Expand, Calibrate process, which first compares an observational emulation against an existing randomized controlled trial (RCT), then uses observed divergence to calibrate a second emulation for a new indication causal effect estimation. While methodologically powerful, BenchExCal is resource intensive and difficult to scale. We introduce TrialCalibre, a conceptualized multiagent system designed to automate and scale the BenchExCal workflow. Our framework features specialized agents such as the Orchestrator, Protocol Design, Data Synthesis, Clinical Validation, and Quantitative Calibration Agents that coordi-nate the the overall process. TrialCalibre incorpo-rates agent learning (e.g., RLHF) and knowledge blackboards to support adaptive, auditable, and transparent causal effect estimation.

</details>


### [39] [Action-Aware Generative Sequence Modeling for Short Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25834)
*Wenhao Li,Zihan Lin,Zhengxiao Guo,Jie Zhou,Shukai Liu,Yongqi Liu,Chuan Luo,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动作感知生成序列网络（A2Gen），通过建模用户行为序列和时间维度上的细微偏好，在快手平台实现日均服务4亿+用户，互动率提升8.1%，留存提升0.162%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二分类推荐模型将短视频视为整体实体，无法捕捉用户对视频不同片段的差异化态度；用户消费是时序过程，行为时机能反映多样化意图，需在时间维度细化用户行为进行建模。

Method: 提出A2Gen框架：1）上下文感知注意力模块（CAM）融合动作序列与物品上下文特征；2）层次序列编码器（HSE）从历史行为学习时序动作模式；3）动作序列自回归生成器（AAG）生成动作序列。将细化后的时序动作链接为序列进行统一处理和预测。

Result: 在快手数据集和天猫公开数据集的离线实验中表现优越；快手平台大规模在线A/B测试显示：用户观看时长提升0.34%，互动率提升8.1%，用户留存（LifeTime-7）提升0.162%，已全流量部署服务超4亿日活用户。

Conclusion: 通过将用户行为在时间维度细化并建模为序列，A2Gen有效捕捉了用户细粒度偏好，在多任务预测中显著优于基线方法，证明了时序动作建模在实际推荐系统中的价值和可扩展性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of the Internet, users have increasingly higher expectations for the recommendation accuracy of online content consumption platforms. However, short videos often contain diverse segments, and users may not hold the same attitude toward all of them. Traditional binary-classification recommendation models, which treat a video as a single holistic entity, face limitations in accurately capturing such nuanced preferences. Considering that user consumption is a temporal process, this paper demonstrates that the timing of user actions can represent diverse intentions through statistical analysis and examination of action patterns. Based on this insight, we propose a novel modeling paradigm: Action-Aware Generative Sequence Network (A2Gen), which refines user actions along the temporal dimension and chains them into sequences for unified processing and prediction. First, we introduce the Context-aware Attention Module (CAM) to model action sequences enriched with item-specific contextual features. Building upon this, we develop the Hierarchical Sequence Encoder (HSE) to learn temporal action patterns from users' historical actions. Finally, through leveraging CAM, we design a module for action sequence generation: the Action-seq Autoregressive Generator (AAG). Extensive offline experiments on the Kuaishou's dataset and the Tmall public dataset demonstrate the superiority of our proposed model. Furthermore, through large-scale online A/B testing deployed on Kuaishou's platform, our model achieves significant improvements over baseline methods in multi-task prediction by leveraging sequential information. Specifically, it yields increases of 0.34% in user watch time, 8.1% in interaction rate, and 0.162% in overall user retention (LifeTime-7), leading to successful deployment across all traffic, serving over 400 million users every day.

</details>


### [40] [Semi-Markov Reinforcement Learning for City-Scale EV Ride-Hailing with Feasibility-Guaranteed Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25848)
*An Nguyen,Hoang Nguyen,Phuong Le,Hung Pham,Cuong Do,Laurent El Ghaoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PD-RSAC方法解决城市级电动汽车网约车队控制问题，在充电器和馈线限制下优化调度、调度和充电决策。通过结合意图投影MILP和Wasserstein鲁棒强化学习，在NYC数据实验中实现122万美元净利润，是基准方法的1.7-2.1倍，同时零违反馈线限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级电动汽车网约车队控制面临多重挑战：需要在充电器和馈线限制下做出调度、重新部署和充电决策，同时应对不确定且空间相关的需求和行程时间。现有方法难以在保证物理可行性的同时实现高效优化，且容易受到分布偏移的影响。

Method: 1) 建立六边形网格半马尔可夫决策过程(半MDP)，包含混合动作(离散的服务、调度、充电动作和连续充电功率)和可变动作持续时间；2) 设计掩码温度退火演员网络生成高层意图，通过时间限制的滚动混合整数线性规划(MILP)在每步严格强制执行荷电状态、端口和馈线约束；3) 采用Wasserstein-1模糊集和图对齐马氏距离度量来缓解分布偏移，优化Soft Actor-Critic代理；4) 架构结合双层GCN编码器、双评论家网络和驱动对手的价值网络。

Result: 在基于纽约出租车数据的大规模电动车队仿真器上实验，PD-RSAC实现最高净利润122万美元，相比Greedy、SAC、MAPPO和MADDPG等基准方法(58-70万美元)提升74-110%，同时保持零馈线限制违反。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功解决了电动车队控制的物理可行性和鲁棒性问题，通过意图投影MILP保证约束满足，通过Wasserstein鲁棒优化应对分布偏移，在实际规模问题上显著优于现有启发式和强化学习方法，证明了该方法在复杂城市环境下车队管理的有效性。

Abstract: We study city-scale control of electric-vehicle (EV) ride-hailing fleets where dispatch, repositioning, and charging decisions must respect charger and feeder limits under uncertain, spatially correlated demand and travel times. We formulate the problem as a hex-grid semi-Markov decision process (semi-MDP) with mixed actions -- discrete actions for serving, repositioning, and charging, together with continuous charging power -- and variable action durations. To guarantee physical feasibility during both training and deployment, the policy learns over high-level intentions produced by a masked, temperature-annealed actor. These intentions are projected at every decision step through a time-limited rolling mixed-integer linear program (MILP) that strictly enforces state-of-charge, port, and feeder constraints. To mitigate distributional shifts, we optimize a Soft Actor--Critic (SAC) agent against a Wasserstein-1 ambiguity set with a graph-aligned Mahalanobis ground metric that captures spatial correlations. The robust backup uses the Kantorovich--Rubinstein dual, a projected subgradient inner loop, and a primal--dual risk-budget update. Our architecture combines a two-layer Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) encoder, twin critics, and a value network that drives the adversary. Experiments on a large-scale EV fleet simulator built from NYC taxi data show that PD--RSAC achieves the highest net profit, reaching \$1.22M, compared with \$0.58M--\$0.70M for strong heuristic, single-agent RL, and multi-agent RL baselines, including Greedy, SAC, MAPPO, and MADDPG, while maintaining zero feeder-limit violations.

</details>


### [41] [ADEMA: A Knowledge-State Orchestration Architecture for Long-Horizon Knowledge Synthesis with LLMAgents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25849)
*Zhou Hanlin,Chan Huah Yong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ADEMA架构，通过显式认知状态管理、双评估治理、检查点恢复等机制解决长周期LLM任务中知识状态漂移、中间承诺隐式化和中断导致的证据链断裂问题，核心贡献是建立了以显式认知状态转换、可追溯产物演进和可恢复连续性为主要设计承诺的知识状态编排架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期LLM任务失败往往不是因为无法获得单个答案，而是因为跨轮次的知识状态漂移、中间承诺保持隐式化、以及中断破坏了演进的证据链。现有方法缺乏对长周期知识合成任务的系统性状态编排支持。

Method: 提出ADEMA知识状态编排架构，包含九个核心机制：显式认知账本、异构双评估治理、自适应任务模式切换、声誉驱动的资源分配、检查点可恢复持久化、分段级记忆凝缩、产物优先组装、最终有效性检查与安全回退。通过四场景展示包、固定60运行机制矩阵、针对性微消融与产物链补充、以及修复的协议级基准进行验证。

Result: 在固定矩阵实验中，移除检查点/恢复功能产生了唯一的无效运行，且发生在中断敏感的恢复条件下。双评估、分段合成和动态治理更应被解释为塑造轨迹纪律、显式产物演进和成本-质量行为的支持性控制机制，而非完成的通用二元先决条件。代码导向评估是最清晰的质量敏感机制模块。

Conclusion: ADEMA作为知识状态编排架构的核心设计承诺是：显式认知状态转换、承载证据的产物演进、以及可恢复连续性。检查点/恢复机制对于任务完成至关重要，而双评估、分段合成和动态治理则是优化控制机制。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM tasks often fail not because a single answer is unattainable, but because knowledge states drift across rounds, intermediate commitments remain implicit, and interruption fractures the evolving evidence chain. This paper presents ADEMA as a knowledge-state orchestration architecture for long-horizon knowledge synthesis rather than as a generic multi-agent runtime. The architecture combines explicit epistemic bookkeeping, heterogeneous dual-evaluator governance, adaptive task-mode switching, reputation-shaped resource allocation, checkpoint-resumable persistence, segment-level memory condensation, artifact-first assembly, and final-validity checking with safe fallback. Evidence is drawn entirely from existing materials: a four-scenario showcase package, a fixed 60-run mechanism matrix, targeted micro-ablation and artifact-chain supplements, and a repaired protocol-level benchmark in which code-oriented evaluation is the clearest quality-sensitive mechanism block. Across the fixed matrix, removing checkpoint/resume produced the only invalid run, and it did so in the interruption-sensitive resume condition. By contrast, dual evaluation, segment synthesis, and dynamic governance are best interpreted as supporting control mechanisms that shape trajectory discipline, explicit artifact progression, and cost-quality behavior rather than as universal binary prerequisites for completion. The contribution is therefore a knowledge-state orchestration architecture in which explicit epistemic state transition, evidence-bearing artifact progression, and recoverable continuity are the primary design commitments.

</details>


### [42] [Recursive Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25917)
*Xiyuan Yang,Jiaru Zou,Rui Pan,Ruizhong Qiu,Pan Lu,Shizhe Diao,Jindong Jiang,Hanghang Tong,Tong Zhang,Markus J. Buehler,Jingrui He,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RecursiveMAS框架，将递归计算从单一模型扩展到多智能体系统，通过递归协作实现性能提升、推理加速和资源节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的递归语言模型证明了在单一模型上通过迭代细化潜在状态可以增强推理能力。本文旨在探索是否能将这种递归缩放原则从单一模型扩展到多智能体系统，即通过递归方式扩展智能体协作本身。

Method: 提出RecursiveMAS框架，将整个多智能体系统视为统一的潜在空间递归计算。通过轻量级RecursiveLink模块将异构智能体连接为协作环路，实现分布内潜在思维生成和跨智能体潜在状态转移。开发了内外循环学习算法，通过递归轮次间的共享梯度信用分配进行迭代式全系统协同优化。

Result: 在9个涵盖数学、科学、医学、搜索和代码生成的基准测试中，与先进的单一/多智能体及递归计算基线相比，RecursiveMAS平均准确率提升8.3%，端到端推理速度提升1.2×-2.4×，Token使用量减少34.6%-75.6%。理论分析证明了其比标准文本型MAS更高效，且递归训练期间梯度稳定。

Conclusion: 研究成功证明了智能体协作本身可以通过递归进行有效缩放。RecursiveMAS框架在多种协作模式下均表现出色，实现了性能、效率和资源利用的同步优化，为多智能体系统的发展提供了新的缩放维度。

Abstract: Recursive or looped language models have recently emerged as a new scaling axis by iteratively refining the same model computation over latent states to deepen reasoning. We extend such scaling principle from a single model to multi-agent systems, and ask: Can agent collaboration itself be scaled through recursion? To this end, we introduce RecursiveMAS, a recursive multi-agent framework that casts the entire system as a unified latent-space recursive computation. RecursiveMAS connects heterogeneous agents as a collaboration loop through the lightweight RecursiveLink module, enabling in-distribution latent thoughts generation and cross-agent latent state transfer. To optimize our framework, we develop an inner-outer loop learning algorithm for iterative whole-system co-optimization through shared gradient-based credit assignment across recursion rounds. Theoretical analyses of runtime complexity and learning dynamics establish that RecursiveMAS is more efficient than standard text-based MAS and maintains stable gradients during recursive training. Empirically, we instantiate RecursiveMAS under 4 representative agent collaboration patterns and evaluate across 9 benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, medicine, search, and code generation. In comparison with advanced single/multi-agent and recursive computation baselines, RecursiveMAS consistently delivers an average accuracy improvement of 8.3%, together with 1.2$\times$-2.4$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and 34.6%-75.6% token usage reduction. Code and Data are provided in https://recursivemas.github.io.

</details>


### [43] [A Quantitative Definition of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10873)
*Kang-Sin Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的通用智能定量定义（智能密度=独立输出对数/总描述长度），区分了“记忆”与“知识”，并反驳了Searle关于“语法不足以产生语义”的论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为任意物理系统提供一个可操作、可量化且基底独立的智能定义，以区分单纯的记忆存储与真正的泛化（知识），并探讨语法与语义之间的关系。

Method: 定义“智能密度”为系统独立输出对数与总描述长度之比：若描述长度随输出增加而增长则为记忆，若描述长度固定而输出数量无限增长则为知识（即实现了泛化）。同时定义输出的“情境性”度量为给定先前输出上下文的条件Kolmogorov复杂度的倒数，以此统一正确性与独立性。

Result: 该定义将智能置于从逻辑门到人脑的连续谱系上，证明了在可界定正确性的领域中，意义是对函数的选择与排序；结合情境性度量，统一了正确性与独立性条件。

Conclusion: 在正确性可界定的任何领域中，所提出的框架反驳了Searle的第三前提（即语法不足以产生语义），证明了语法系统可以实现语义功能。

Abstract: We propose an operational, quantitative definition of intelligence for arbitrary physical systems. The intelligence density of a system is the ratio of the logarithm of its independent outputs to its total description length. A system memorizes if its description length grows with its output count; it knows if its description length remains fixed while its output count diverges. The criterion for knowing is generalization. A system knows its domain if a single finite mechanism can produce correct outputs across an unbounded range of inputs, rather than storing each answer individually. The definition places intelligence on a substrate-independent continuum from logic gates to brains. We then argue that meaning over a domain is a selection and ordering of functions that produces correct outputs where correctness is specifiable. We also define a measure of contextuality of an output as the inverse of its conditional Kolmogorov complexity given the context of prior outputs, which unifies correctness and independence into a single condition. Together, these refute Searle's third premise, that syntax is insufficient for semantics, over any domain where correctness is specifiable.

</details>

### [44] [Cloud to Edge: Benchmarking LLM Inference On Hardware-Accelerated Single-Board Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24785)
*Harri Renney,Fouad Trad,Michael Mattarock,Zena Wood*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多维基准测试方法，评估单板计算机上大语言模型的推理性能与硬件效率，发现硬件加速器能显著提升性能，并为隐私敏感环境下的边缘部署提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统云端部署大语言模型在数据隐私、延迟和成本方面存在挑战，尤其在运营技术和国防环境中问题突出。虽然模型蒸馏、量化和边缘加速器的发展使得在单板计算机上进行本地推理成为可能，但由于配置空间的高维性，难以在没有结构化评估的情况下识别最优部署方案。现有的LLM边缘基准测试存在依赖CPU推理、单板计算机覆盖不足、评估任务缺乏多维硬件效率评估等问题。

Method: 提出一种多维基准测试方法，联合评估推理性能和硬件效率。该方法在四种适合IoT的边缘平台配置上进行测试，使用配备最新硬件加速器（如NPU和GPU）的单板计算机，并通过多维度评估量化功耗效率、设备物理尺寸与token吞吐量之间的权衡关系。

Result: 实验结果揭示了使用NPU和GPU等硬件加速器的优势，通过多维评估量化了功耗效率、设备物理尺寸和token吞吐量之间的权衡关系，为在隐私敏感和连接受限环境（如无人驾驶车辆和便携式加固操作）中部署生成式AI提供了实用指导。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了硬件加速器在边缘LLM部署中的重要性，并通过多维基准测试方法为实际应用场景提供了硬件选择和配置优化的指导，有助于推动隐私敏感和连接受限环境中的生成式AI应用发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly capable at small parameter scales. At the same time, conventional cloud-centric deployment introduces challenges around data privacy, latency, and cost that are acute in operational technology and defence environments. Advances in model distillation, quantisation, and affordable edge accelerators now make local LLM inference on single-board computers feasible, but the high dimensionality of the configuration space makes identifying optimal deployments difficult without structured evaluation. Existing LLM-specific edge benchmarking efforts rely on CPU-only inference, poor coverage of genuine single-board computers, and generic evaluation tasks that lack multi-dimensional assessment of hardware effectiveness. This paper proposes a multi-dimensional benchmarking methodology that jointly evaluates inference performance and hardware efficiency across four IoT-suitable edge platform configurations testing single-board computers with the latest available hardware accelerators. Our results reveal the benefits of using hardware accelerators such as NPUs and GPUs, along with multi-dimensional evaluations quantifying the trade-offs between power efficiency, physical device size and token throughput; offering practical guidance for deploying generative AI in privacy-sensitive and connectivity-limited environments such as unmanned vehicles and portable, ruggedised operations.

</details>


### [45] [Opto-Atomic Spatio-Temporal Holographic Correlators for High-Speed 3D CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24800)
*Xi Shen,Bowen Qi,Tabassom Hamidfar,Selim M. Shahriar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种光电混合架构，利用光-原子时空全息相关器(SHC)处理3D CNN中计算密集的卷积层，在实现59.72%分类准确率的同时，预计可达125,000帧/秒的处理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维卷积神经网络(3D CNN)在视频识别任务中表现出色，但其计算复杂度随规模呈立方增长，导致在传统硅基硬件上面临时间和能效方面的严重挑战。

Method: 提出一种光电混合架构，将计算密集的3D卷积层委托给光-原子时空全息相关器(SHC)。该系统将时间信息以原子相干的形式存储在不均匀展宽的冷铷-85原子阵列中，并结合传统的2D空间相关器，同时执行空间和时间上的相关运算。

Result: 在四类人体动作数据集上实现了59.72%的分类准确率，使用了并行大尺度核（空间30x40像素，时间8帧），并预计潜在运行速度可达每秒125,000帧。

Conclusion: 该方法通过光电混合架构为大规模加速视频分类提供了一条可行的技术路径，有效解决了传统硬件面临的计算效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Three-dimensional convolutional neural networks (3D CNNs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in video recognition tasks by processing both spatial and temporal features. However, the cubic scaling of computational complexity poses significant time and energy efficiency challenges for conventional silicon-based hardware. To address this, we propose a hybrid optoelectronic architecture that delegates the computationally intensive 3D convolutional layer to an opto-atomic Spatio-temporal Holographic Correlator (STHC). This system stores temporal information as atomic coherence in an array of inhomogeneously broadened cold Rubidium-85 atoms and combines a traditional 2D spatial correlator to perform correlation in both space and time simultaneously. Our results on a four-class human action dataset demonstrate a classification accuracy of 59.72% using parallel large-scale kernels (30X40 pixels spatially, 8 frames temporally), with potential operating speeds projected up to 125,000 frames per second. This approach offers a pathway to massively accelerated video classification through a hybrid architecture.

</details>


### [46] [Salca: A Sparsity-Aware Hardware Accelerator for Efficient Long-Context Attention Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24820)
*Wang Fan,Wei Cao,Xi Zha,Kedi Ma,MingQian Sun,Jialin Chen,Fengzhe Zhang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个支持长上下文推理的ASIC加速器，通过软硬件协同设计实现双压缩动态稀疏注意力机制，相比A100实现3.82倍加速和74.19倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的长上下文能力虽强，但带来严重的硬件挑战：计算和内存占用随序列长度线性增长，解码阶段持续访问海量KV缓存导致带宽和计算压力剧增。现有加速器主要针对短上下文设计，处理长上下文时性能显著下降。

Method: 软件方面提出双压缩动态稀疏注意力，结合超低精度量化与特征稀疏性最小化预测开销，采用硬件友好的近似Top-K选择将过滤复杂度从O(n log k)降至O(n)。硬件方面深度优化计算和内存访问，解决稀疏注意力与长上下文复杂交互的瓶颈，建立性能模型推导最优协同设计方案，采用全流水线并行架构。

Result: 实验表明该设计相比A100实现3.82倍加速和74.19倍能效提升；相比SOTA加速器，吞吐量至少提升3.5倍，能效提升2.08倍，是首个高效支持长上下文推理的ASIC加速器，即使对长序列也能保持O(n)效率。

Conclusion: 通过软硬件协同设计的双压缩动态稀疏注意力加速器成功解决了长上下文推理的硬件瓶颈，为长上下文大语言模型的高效部署提供了可行的ASIC解决方案，在性能和能效上均取得显著突破。

Abstract: Long contexts improve capabilities of large language models but pose serious hardware challenges: compute and memory footprints grow linearly with sequence length. Particularly, the decoding phase continuously accesses massive KV cache, dramatically increasing bandwidth and computing pressure. Existing accelerators are primarily designed and evaluated for short contexts. They suffer from significant performance degradation when processing long contexts. To bridge this gap, we identify the major bottleneck and present a hardware accelerator for long context attention decoding via hardware-software co-design. On the software side, we propose dual-compression dynamic sparse attention. It combines ultra-low-precision quantization with feature sparsity to minimize prediction overhead. A hardware-friendly approximate Top-K selection further reduces filter complexity from $O(n \log k)$ to $O(n)$. On the hardware side, we deeply optimize compute and memory access to tackle bottlenecks from intricate interplay between sparse attention and long contexts, and establish a performance model to derive the optimal co-design scheme. The resulting hardware adopts a fully pipelined parallel architecture and achieves $O(n)$ efficiency even for long sequences. Experiments show that our design delivers $3.82\times$ speedup and $74.19\times$ energy efficiency over A100. Compared to SOTA accelerators, this is the first ASIC accelerator that efficiently supports long context inference, with at least $3.5\times$ higher throughput and $2.08\times$ better energy efficiency.

</details>


### [47] [Hardware Generation and Exploration of Lookup Table-Based Accelerators for 1.58-bit LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25183)
*Robin Geens,Joran Heldens,Joren Dumoulin,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个开源的三值权重LUT加速器硬件生成框架及分析模型，通过系统化的设计空间探索，证明了激活数据类型是架构选择的决定性因素，并实现了相比乘法器基线2.2倍的面积缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三值权重量化（如BitNet b1.58）能有效缓解LLM推理中的内存带宽瓶颈，但现有计算平台缺乏对三值权重算术的原生支持。现有的LUT架构设计依赖启发式参数选择，缺乏对架构权衡的系统理解，且不同加速器间的实现不一致阻碍了公平比较。

Method: 形式化定义了三值LUT加速器的设计空间，并提出了一个开源硬件生成器，配合经过TSMC 16nm工艺流片验证的分析成本模型。该框架覆盖完整架构空间，支持快速设计空间探索，并为公平的跨设计评估建立了统一基准。

Result: 揭示了最优架构由激活数据类型决定：LUT复用对高成本算术（如FP16）收益显著，但对小整数类型收益递减；最大化核心尺寸比高度分块方案能持续提升面积密度。优化设计相比乘法器基线实现2.2倍面积缩减；修正现有SOTA实现的次优参数可带来高达1.2倍的面积改善。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了三值LUT加速器系统化设计方法的空白，挑战了现有文献中的多项假设与设计选择，为未来架构设计提供了经过验证的分析框架和明确的优化指导原则。

Abstract: Ternary weight quantization (e.g., BitNet b1.58) offers a promising path to mitigate the memory bandwidth bottleneck in Large Language Model (LLM) inference. However, conventional compute platforms lack native support for ternary-weight arithmetic, often relying on inefficient dequantization. Lookup table (LUT)-based hardware architectures provide an effective alternative by replacing multiplications with conditional additions, but their design space remains largely unexplored. Existing designs rely on heuristic parameter selection, lacking a systematic understanding of the architectural trade-offs. This work addresses this gap by formalizing the design space of ternary LUT-based accelerators and presenting an open-source hardware generator coupled with an analytical cost model, validated against synthesis in TSMC 16nm technology. By spanning the full architectural space, this framework not only enables rapid design space exploration but also establishes a common footing for fair cross-design evaluation, which was previously hindered by inconsistent instantiations across published accelerators. Using this framework, we challenge several assumptions and design choices in recent literature. We demonstrate that the optimal architecture is fundamentally governed by the activation data type: while LUT-based reuse offers significant gains for high-cost arithmetic (e.g., FP16), it yields diminishing returns for small integer types. Furthermore, we show that maximizing core size consistently improves area density compared to highly tiled approaches. Our optimized designs achieve a 2.2x area reduction compared to multiplier-based baselines. Moreover, by benchmarking state-of-the-art implementations against our model, we reveal that correcting suboptimal parameters yields up to a 1.2x area improvement.

</details>


### [48] [How Can Reinforcement Learning Achieve Expert-level Placement?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25191)
*Ruo-Tong Chen,Ke Xue,Chengrui Gao,Yunqi Shi,Tian Xu,Peng Xie,Siyuan Xu,Mingxuan Yuan,Chao Qian,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从专家布局中学习隐式奖励的芯片布局方法，通过逆向推导专家轨迹并训练奖励模型，弥补了强化学习方法在训练中仅优化线长而无法达到专家质量的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的芯片布局方法在训练时主要聚焦于线长优化，导致其生成的布局往往无法达到专家级质量。作者发现奖励设计是造成这一性能差距的主要原因，因此提出绕过复杂的奖励形式化过程，直接从专家布局中学习奖励模型。

Method: 首先从最终的专家布局出发，逆向推断逐步的专家轨迹；然后将这些轨迹作为示范或偏好数据，训练一个能够捕捉专家结果中潜在隐式奖励的模型。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能够高效地从少量（甚至单个）设计中学习，并且对未见过的设计案例具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过从专家布局中直接学习隐式奖励，该方法有效弥补了现有RL方法在奖励设计上的不足，能够生成更高质量的芯片布局，具有良好的数据效率和泛化性能。

Abstract: Chip placement is a critical step in physical design. While reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods have recently emerged, their training primarily focuses on wirelength optimization, and therefore often fail to achieve expert-quality layouts. We identify the reward design as the primary cause for the performance gap with experts, and instead of formalizing intricate processes, we circumvent this by directly learning from expert layouts to derive a reward model. Our approach starts from the final expert layouts to infer step-by-step expert trajectories. Using these trajectories as demonstrations or preferences, we train a model that captures the latent implicit rewards in expert results. Experiments show that our framework can efficiently learn from even a single design and generalize well to unseen cases.

</details>


### [49] [FusionCIM: Accelerating LLM Inference with Fusion-Driven Computing-in-Memory Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25317)
*Zihao Xuan,Jia Chen,Yewen Li,Wei Xuan,Hegan Chen,Xiao Huo,Fengbin Tu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了FusionCIM，一种面向LLM推理的算子融合驱动存内计算加速器，相比现有SOTA CIM设计实现了最高3.86倍节能和1.98倍加速，系统级能效达29.4 TOPS/W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理面临计算量大和访存开销高的问题。现有的存内计算（CIM）加速器在处理Transformer中的注意力机制时，存在数据重复加载、片上数据复用率低以及非线性操作融合开销大等瓶颈。为了进一步提升LLM推理的效率与扩展性，需要从架构层面针对算子融合进行专门优化。

Method: 提出了FusionCIM架构，包含三项核心创新：(1) 混合CIM流水线架构，将QKT计算映射到基于内积的CIM（IP-CIM），将PV聚合映射到基于外积的CIM（OP-CIM），实现高效的矩阵乘法融合；(2) QO-stationary数据流，消除了CIM中KV的重复加载以及转置融合时缓冲区中对K矩阵的访问，显著提升片上数据复用；(3) 模式感知在线Softmax机制，利用注意力分数的分布规律来减少非线性融合中的指数重缩放开销。

Result: 在LLaMA-3模型上的实验结果表明，与现有最先进的CIM设计相比，FusionCIM实现了最高3.86倍的能量节省和1.98倍的加速，系统级能效达到29.4 TOPS/W。

Conclusion: FusionCIM证明了通过算子融合驱动的混合CIM架构设计，可以有效解决LLM推理中的数据移动和计算效率瓶颈，为构建高效能、可扩展的LLM加速器提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose FusionCIM, an operator-fusion-driven compute-in-memory (CIM) accelerator architecture for efficient and scalable LLM inference, with three key innovations: (1) a hybrid CIM pipeline architecture that maps QKT computation on inner-product-based CIM (IP-CIM) and PV aggregation on outer-product-based CIM (OP-CIM) for efficient matrix multiplications fusion; (2) a QO-stationary dataflow that eliminates repeated KV loading in CIM and K-matrix access in buffer under transpose fusion, significantly improving data reuse on chip; and (3) a pattern-aware online-softmax mechanism that exploits distribution regularities of attention scores to reduce exponential rescaling overhead for non-linear fusion. Experimental results on LLaMA-3 model show that FusionCIM achieves up to 3.86x energy saving, and 1.98x speedup compared with prior SOTA CIM-based designs with 29.4 TOPS/W energy efficiency at the system level.

</details>


### [50] [AHASD: Asynchronous Heterogeneous Architecture for LLM Adaptive Drafting Speculative Decoding on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25326)
*Ma zirui,Fan Zhihua,Li Wenxing,Wu Haibin,Zhang Fulin,Ye Xiaochun,Li Wenming*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AHASD，一种面向移动端单NPU-PIM系统的任务级异步推测解码架构，通过熵-历史感知的起草控制和时间感知的预验证控制，在PIM上并行起草、NPU上验证，实现了高达4.2倍的吞吐量和5.6倍的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码通过小模型起草、大模型批量验证来提升大语言模型推理效率。但在移动端单NPU-PIM系统上，自适应起草推理面临传统算子级同步执行的空闲开销，以及异步执行中因草稿长度波动导致的计算浪费问题。现有方法难以高效支持移动端异构系统上的自适应起草推理。

Method: 提出AHASD架构，实现任务级DLM-TLM解耦，使PIM上的起草与单个NPU上的验证并行执行。引入两个关键机制：（1）熵-历史感知起草控制，动态管理自适应起草算法执行，抑制低置信度草稿的无效起草；（2）时间感知预验证控制，优化预验证时机。在LPDDR5-PIM中集成注意力算法单元和门控任务调度单元，实现注意力链接本地化和亚微秒级任务切换。

Result: 在不同LLM和自适应起草算法上的实验表明，相比纯GPU基线，AHASD实现最高4.2倍吞吐量和5.6倍能效提升；相比最先进的GPU+PIM基线，实现1.5倍吞吐量和1.24倍能效提升，硬件开销低于DRAM面积的3%。

Conclusion: AHASD成功解决了移动端单NPU-PIM系统上推测解码的效率问题，通过任务级异步架构和智能控制机制，显著提升了推理吞吐量和能效，为移动端大语言模型高效推理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding enhances the inference efficiency of large language models (LLMs) by generating drafts using a small draft language model (DLM) and verifying them in batches with a large target language model (TLM). However, adaptive drafting inference on a mobile single-NPU-PIM system faces idle overhead in traditional operator-level synchronous execution and wasted computation in asynchronous execution due to fluctuations in draft length. This paper introduces AHASD, a task-level asynchronous mobile NPU-PIM heterogeneous architecture for speculative decoding. Notably, AHASD achieves parallel drafting on the PIM and verification on a single NPU through task-level DLM-TLM decoupling and specifically, it incorporates Entropy-History-Aware Drafting Control and Time-Aware Pre-Verification Control to dynamically manage adaptive drafting algorithm execution and pre-verification timing, suppressing invalid drafting based on low-confidence drafts. Additionally, AHASD integrates Attention Algorithm Units and Gated Task Scheduling Units within LPDDR5-PIM to enable attention link localization and sub-microsecond task switching on the PIM side. Experimental results for different LLMs and adaptive drafting algorithms show that AHASD achieves up to 4.2$\times$ in throughput and 5.6$\times$ in energy efficiency improvements over a GPU-only baseline, and 1.5$\times$ in throughput and 1.24$\times$ in energy efficiency gains over the state-of-the-art GPU+PIM baseline, with hardware overhead below 3\% of the DRAM area.

</details>


### [51] [RecFlash: Fast Recommendation System on In-Storage Computing with Frequency-Based Data Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25338)
*Jangho Baik,Sunghyun Kim,Gisan Ji,Wonbo Shim,Sungju Ryu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了RecFlash，一种基于NAND闪存存内计算（ISC）的推荐推理加速器，通过数据重映射算法优化了不规则的随机内存访问模式，显著降低了延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统需要处理大量用户数据，实时处理困难。NAND闪存具有大容量优势，适合加速推荐推理。然而，推荐系统的不规则随机内存访问模式导致从NAND闪存加载数据时利用率低，内部带宽浪费严重，性能下降。

Method: 提出RecFlash加速器，采用数据重映射算法，针对推荐系统不规则随机访问模式进行优化，基于NAND闪存存内计算架构设计。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有NAND闪存ISC架构相比，延迟降低高达81%，能耗降低高达91.9%。

Conclusion: RecFlash通过数据重映射有效解决了推荐系统中随机访问导致的带宽利用率低问题，显著提升了推理性能和能效，验证了NAND闪存ISC在推荐任务加速方面的有效性。

Abstract: Recommendation system has gained a large popularity for a variety of personalized suggestion tasks, but the ever-increasing number of user data makes real-time processing of recommendation systems difficult. NAND flash memory-based in-storage computing scheme can be one of favorable candidates among the various acceleration approaches because the flash memory typically has a larger memory capacity than the other memory types, so it can efficiently handle a large amount of user data for the recommendation inference services. However, different from other neural network applications where data is sequentially fetched from memory, the recommendation system shows the irregular random memory access pattern. Hence, most of the data loaded from the NAND flash array to the page buffer are not used, so a large portion of the internal bandwidth is underutilized, which degrades the performance on the inference acceleration of the recommendation tasks. In this paper, we propose RecFlash, a fast recommendation inference accelerator utilizing a data remapping algorithm with NAND flash-based in-storage computing (ISC). The experimental results show that our proposed method improves the latency and energy consumption by up to 81% and 91.9%, respectively, over the existing NAND flash-based ISC architecture.

</details>


### [52] [TetrisG-SDK: Efficient Convolutional Layer Mapping with Adaptive Windows and Grouped Convolutions for Fast In-Memory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25377)
*Ke Dong,Kejie Huang,Tao Luo,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TetrisG-SDK框架，通过自适应窗口和多宏并行优化提升CIM硬件上卷积层的映射性能，在多种CNN模型上实现了显著的加速和能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SDK映射方法（如VWC-SDK）仅针对单个CIM宏进行优化，未探索宏间并行性，且映射方法仍不够优化，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出TetrisG-SDK框架：1）采用自适应窗口策略以容纳更多输入通道、提高阵列利用率并适应不同通道深度；2）在固定硬件预算下跨多个CIM宏搜索最优窗口配置以降低计算延迟；3）引入分组卷积以减少计算周期并保持近无损精度；4）集成CIM硬件仿真器以准确评估系统级指标。

Result: 相比单宏VWC-SDK，在CNN8、GoogLeNet Inception和DenseNet40上分别实现1.2x、1.3x和1.3x加速；在仿真器上部署后系统级延迟和能耗分别降低：CNN8为2.4x和1.7x、Inception为1.3x和1.2x、DenseNet40为1.3x和1.6x；利用宏级并行时，相比非分组版本，EDAP分别降低70%（CNN8）、68%（Inception）和36%（DenseNet40）。

Conclusion: TetrisG-SDK是高效实现CIM硬件上卷积层映射的有效解决方案，能够显著提升性能和能效。

Abstract: Shifted-and-Duplicated-Kernel (SDK) mapping has emerged as an effective strategy to accelerate convolutional layers on compute-in-memory (CIM) hardware. However, existing SDK variants (e.g., VWC-SDK) merely optimize mapping for a single CIM macro, leaving inter-macro parallelism unexplored. Moreover, their mapping methodologies are still suboptimal. To address these limitations, we present TetrisG-SDK, a novel framework that employs adaptive windows to boost mapping performance. The proposed windows accommodate more input channels, increase array utilization at marginal space, and adapt to different channel depths. More importantly, TetrisG-SDK reduces compute latency by searching for optimal window configurations across multiple CIM macros with a fixed hardware budget. Besides, it incorporates grouped convolution to further decrease computing cycles while maintaining near-lossless model accuracy. In addition, TetrisG-SDK integrates a validated CIM hardware simulator to provide accurate system-/application-level estimations of latency, area and energy.
  Compared to the single-macro VWC-SDK, the proposed framework achieves a speed-up by 1.2x, 1.3x, and 1.3x for CNN8, GoogLeNet Inception, and DenseNet40 models, respectively. When deployed on the simulator, it reduces system-level latency and energy by 2.4x and 1.7x for CNN8, 1.3x and 1.2x for Inception, and 1.3x and 1.6x for DenseNet40, respectively. When leveraging macro-level parallelism, TetrisG-SDK reduces the Energy-Delay-Area-Product (EDAP) by 70% for CNN8, 68% for Inception, and 36% for DenseNet40 compared to its non-grouped counterpart. These results manifest that TetrisG-SDK is a promising solution to efficiently mapping convolutional layers on CIM hardware.

</details>


### [53] [NVLLM: A 3D NAND-Centric Architecture Enabling Edge on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25699)
*Mingbo Hao,Changwei Yan,Haoyu Cui,Zhihao Yan,Yizhi Ding,Zhangrui Qian,Weiwei Shan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出NVLLM，一种以3D NAND为中心的LLM推理架构，通过将FFN计算卸载至Flash并在轻量级CMOS逻辑上执行注意力机制，实现了显著的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的快速增长，在资源受限的边缘设备上进行高吞吐量、内存容量密集型推理的需求日益增加。现有的基于GPU的外核推理和类SSD加速器受限于DRAM绑定的权重移动和低效的存储访问粒度，而单批次解码在根本上仍是内存受限的。

Method: NVLLM采用晶圆对晶圆堆叠技术，将多层平面3D NAND与计算流水线、纠错码（ECC）单元和缓冲区紧密集成。该架构将前馈网络（FFN）计算卸载至Flash中执行，而注意力机制则在带有外部DRAM的轻量级CMOS逻辑上执行。GEMM/GEMV操作被分解为点积原语，由乱序PE通道执行，直接对原始NAND读取进行操作并集成ECC。注意力权重保留在DRAM中，KV缓存感知调度器在上下文长度增长时维持吞吐量。

Result: 在OPT和LLaMA模型（参数量达30B）上的评估显示，NVLLM相比基于A800的外核推理实现了16.7倍至37.9倍的加速，相比类SSD设计实现了高达4.7倍的加速，且CMOS面积开销仅为2.7%。

Conclusion: NVLLM成功展示了以3D NAND为中心的推理架构在解决LLM边缘设备推理的内存瓶颈问题上的有效性，通过创新的计算存储集成设计，在极低的面积开销下实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLMs demands high-throughput, memory-capacity-intensive inference on resource-constrained edge devices, where single-batch decoding remains fundamentally memory-bound. Existing out-of-core GPU-based and SSD-like accelerators are limited by DRAM-bound weight movement and inefficient storage access granularity. We present NVLLM, a 3D NAND-centric inference architecture that offloads feed-forward network (FFN) computation into the Flash while executing attention on lightweight CMOS logic with external DRAM. Through wafer-to-wafer stacking, NVLLM tightly integrates multi-plane 3D NAND with compute pipelines, error correction code (ECC) units, and buffers, enabling page-level FFN weight access without DRAM traversal. All GEMM/GEMV operations are decomposed into dot-product primitives executed by out-of-order PE lanes, operating directly on raw NAND reads with integrated ECC. Attention weights remain in DRAM, and a KV-cache-aware scheduler sustains throughput as the context length grows. Evaluated on OPT and LLaMA models with up to 30B parameters, NVLLM achieves a 16.7$\times$--37.9$\times$ speedup over A800-based out-of-core inference and up to 4.7$\times$ speedup over SSD-like designs, with only 2.7\% CMOS area overhead.

</details>


### [54] [At the Edge of the Heart: ULP FPGA-Based CNN for On-Device Cardiac Feature Extraction in Smart Health Sensors for Astronauts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25799)
*Kazi Mohammad Abidur Rahman,Davis Rakhshan,Philipp Lütke,Laura Harms,Ulf Kulau*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超低功耗FPGA的CNN解决方案，用于资源受限可穿戴设备上的实时心震图（SCG）特征分类，在仅消耗8.55 mW功率的情况下实现了98%的验证精度，适用于空间环境等电池供电场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着载人航天飞行任务的加速推进以及地面关键健康监测需求的增长，资源极其受限的可穿戴健康传感器对可靠、实时特征提取提出了前所未有的要求，迫切需要一种能够在低功耗条件下实现高性能监测的解决方案。

Method: 采用量化感知训练与脉动阵列加速器相结合的方法，在Lattice iCE40UP5K FPGA上实现了纯整数推理的卷积神经网络（CNN）；该FPGA因其高能效和抗辐射能力，特别适合空间环境中的电池供电部署。

Result: 所提出的实现在验证集上达到了98%的准确率，功耗仅为8.55 mW，推理时间为95.5 ms，硬件资源占用极少（仅需2,861个LUT和7个DSP模块）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在资源受限的硬件上实现完全设备端的SCG心脏特征提取是可行的，这为长期空间任务中宇航员的节能、自主健康监测提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: The convergence of accelerating human spaceflight ambitions and critical terrestrial health monitoring demands is driving unprecedented requirements for reliable, real-time feature extraction on extremely resource-constrained wearable health sensors. We present an ultra-low-power (ULP) Field-Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) based solution for real-time Seismocardiography (SCG) feature classification using Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). Our approach combines quantization-aware training with a systolic-array accelerator to enable efficient integer-only inference on the Lattice iCE40UP5K FPGA, which offers an ideal platform for battery-powered deployments -- particularly in space environments -- thanks to its power efficiency and radiation resilience. The implementation achieves a validation accuracy of 98% while consuming only 8.55 mW, completing inference in 95.5 ms with minimal hardware resources (2,861 LUTs and 7 DSP blocks). These results demonstrate that fully on-device SCG-based cardiac feature extraction is feasible on resource-constrained hardware, enabling energy-efficient, autonomous health monitoring for astronauts in long-duration space missions.

</details>
