### [55] [On the Benefits of Traffic "Reprofiling" -- The Multiple Hops Case -- Part II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24930)
*Jiaming Qiu,Roch Guerin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过流量整形（重配置）与简单调度器（静态优先级、FIFO）协同优化的方法，在降低带宽消耗的同时实现硬时延保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在汽车系统、航空电子、工业控制等应用场景中，分组网络提供硬时延保证的需求日益增长。传统观点认为需要复杂调度器才能实现此类保证，而本研究旨在探索如何利用简单的静态优先级和FIFO调度器，通过流重配置技术，以更少的带宽资源实现相同的时延保证目标。

Method: 建立了一个联合优化框架，将流的重配置（即主动将流量整形为不同的流量特征）与简单调度器的配置进行协同优化；针对该优化问题开发了高效求解算法。研究通过在现实网络拓扑和合成拓扑上的广泛评估验证了方法的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，流重配置技术在简单调度器上同样有效，能够在保证硬时延的前提下减少带宽消耗。同时发现了一个直观的耦合关系：调度器的能力越强，其利用复杂重配置方案的能力也相应增强。

Conclusion: 简单的调度器结合流重配置技术可以有效地提供硬时延保证，且具有带宽效率优势。调度器能力与重配置方案复杂度之间存在耦合关系，这为网络设备设计中调度器复杂度与流量管理策略之间的权衡提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Delivering hard delay guarantees over packet networks is increasingly important to applications ranging from automotive systems, avionics, industrial control, etc. Traffic control and schedulers play an essential role in enforcing such guarantees. In this paper, we focus on ``simple'' static priority and FIFO schedulers, and explore how reprofiling flows entering the network, i.e., proactively shaping them to a different traffic profile, can deliver delay guarantees with less bandwidth. To that end, we formulate a joint optimization framework and develop efficient algorithms to solve it. Extensive evaluations across both realistic and synthetic topologies demonstrate that, as with more sophisticated schedulers, reprofiling flows is beneficial. They also highlight an intuitive coupling between a scheduler's capability and its ability to leverage more complex reprofiling solutions.

</details>


### [56] [Optimization of Model Splitting, Placement, and Chaining for Multi-hop Split Learning and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25197)
*Takanori Hara,Masahiro Sasabe*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于业务功能链（SFC）的多跳分裂学习/推理架构，并通过整数线性规划（ILP）模型和块坐标下降（BCD）启发式算法联合优化模型分割、部署和数据路由，以最小化端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受SFC概念的启发，为了支持分布式AI应用在多跳网络中有效路由分裂数据，作者提出了基于SFC的多跳分裂学习（MSL）和分裂推理（MSI）架构。然而，多跳环境带来了新的挑战，包括确定最佳分割点、将子模型部署到合适的计算节点以及通过底层通信网络路由分裂数据同时满足服务要求。

Method: 作者构建了一个整数线性规划（ILP）模型，联合优化基于SFC的MSL/MSI架构中的模型分割、部署和链路（数据路由），目标是使端到端推理或训练延迟最小化。此外，为高效求解该问题，提出了基于块坐标下降（BCD）的启发式算法。

Result: 通过综合评估，证明了所提出的ILP模型和BCD启发式算法在最小化端到端延迟方面的有效性和特性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了多跳分裂学习和推理中的模型分割、部署和路由联合优化问题，为分布式AI应用在复杂网络环境中的部署提供了有效的解决方案。所提出的算法能够高效求解，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Service Function Chaining (SFC) establishes efficient communication paths by ensuring that traffic traverses a predefined sequence of network functions in a specified order to meet particular service requirements. Inspired by this concept, we have proposed an SFC-based architecture for multi-hop split learning (MSL) and split inference (MSI), facilitating distributed AI applications to effectively route smashed data across multi-hop networks. However, the multi-hop environment presents new challenges, including (1) determining optimal cut points, (2) deploying split sub-models on appropriate computing nodes, and (3) routing smashed data through the underlying communication networks while adhering to service requirements. To address these challenges, we formulate an Integer Linear Programming (ILP) model to jointly optimize model splitting, placement, and chaining (data routing) in the SFC-based MSL/MSI architecture, aiming to minimize end-to-end inference or training latency. Additionally, we propose a Block Coordinate Descent (BCD)-based heuristic algorithm to efficiently solve the problem. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness and characteristics of the proposed formulation and algorithm.

</details>


### [57] [Probing for Better Age of Information in Energy-Harvesting Random Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25479)
*Ziyi Li,Fangming Zhao,Howard H. Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了能量收集随机接入网络中信道探测与预留机制对信息年龄(AoI)的影响，发现允许额外竞争的全活跃节点竞争(AUC)方案能实现最低AoI，挑战了严格避碰总是最优的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在能量收集(EH)随机接入网络中，每个源节点仅依赖收集的能量进行状态更新。为减少碰撞，节点可在传输前消耗少量能量发送探测信号，成功的探测可预留信道。传统观点认为在能量受限系统中应严格避免碰撞，但本文旨在探究信道探测与预留机制对AoI的影响，并寻找更优的接入策略。

Method: 提出了三种探测失败后的处理方案：(1)严格避免自由竞争(SAFC)，节点保持静默；(2)预留节点竞争(RUC)，节点以一定概率尝试数据传输；(3)全活跃节点竞争(AUC)，所有能量充足的节点均可竞争。推导了这三种方案下网络平均AoI的闭式表达式，并通过仿真验证了理论结果。

Result: AUC方案始终实现最低的AoI，因为它缩短了将收集能量转化为成功更新的等待时间。这一发现挑战了在能量受限系统中严格避碰总是最优的传统观点，因为允许额外竞争可以有效地将探测开销分摊到更多的传输机会上。与支持EH的时隙ALOHA相比，基于探测的接入在能量受限情况下显著优于直接传输。

Conclusion: 信道探测是提高信息新鲜度的有效方法，在能量受限场景中显著优于直接传输。允许适度竞争的AUC方案比严格避碰方案更能优化AoI性能，表明在能量收集网络中，平衡探测开销与传输机会比单纯避免碰撞更为重要。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the impact of channel probing and reservation on the Age of Information (AoI) in energy-harvesting (EH) random access networks, where each source relies solely on harvested energy for status updating. To mitigate collisions, each node may expend a small amount of energy to send a probing signal before transmission, and a successful probe reserves the channel in the current slot. If probing fails, the node can either remain silent, termed strict avoid free competition (SAFC), attempt data transmission with a certain probability, termed reserved nodes competition (RUC), or adopt all-active nodes competition (AUC), where all energy-sufficient nodes may contend regardless of whether they probed. We derive closed-form expressions for the network-average AoI under these three schemes and validate them via simulations. The results show that AUC consistently achieves the lowest AoI by shortening the waiting time to convert harvested energy into successful updates. This finding challenges the conventional wisdom that strict collision avoidance is always optimal in energy-constrained systems, since allowing additional contention can effectively amortize probing overhead across more transmission opportunities. Comparisons with EH-enabled slotted ALOHA further show that probing-based access significantly outperforms direct transmission in energy-constrained regimes, highlighting channel probing as an effective approach to improving freshness.

</details>


### [58] [Assistants, Not Architects: The Role of LLMs in Networked Systems Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25506)
*Pratyush Sahu,Rahul Bothra,Venkat Arun,Brighten Godfrey,Akshay Narayan,Ahmed Saeed*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLMs在网络系统架构设计任务中存在可靠性问题，并提出Kepler框架，通过结合专家驱动的规范与SMT优化来实现可解释、系统化的架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络系统架构设计需在庞大的组合空间中平衡性能、成本与可部署性等目标，并满足兼容性与资源约束。架构师通常依赖零散的经验法则，而LLMs在此任务中虽能生成看似合理的配置，却常忽略关键约束、编码错误假设且难以纠正，且大规模迭代验证成本高昂或不可行。

Method: 提出Kepler轻量级推理框架，将系统、硬件与工作负载的需求、不兼容性及定性权衡编码为约束，采用SMT求解器合成可行设计并优化用户定义目标；在抽象层面捕获经验法则而非详细行为，实现可处理性并提供决策解释。

Result: 实验与案例研究表明，Kepler能发现LLMs遗漏的关键交互，支持系统性且可解释的设计探索，生成满足约束的优化架构方案。

Conclusion: Kepler弥补了LLMs在架构推理中的不足，证明了结合结构化专家知识与SMT优化方法的可行性与有效性，为网络系统架构设计提供了一种可靠、可解释的自动化方案。

Abstract: Designing the architecture of modern networked systems requires navigating a large, combinatorial space of hardware, systems, and configuration choices with complex cross-layer interactions. Architects must balance competing objectives such as performance, cost, and deployability while satisfying compatibility and resource constraints, often relying on scattered rules-of-thumb drawn from benchmarks, papers, documentation, and expert experience. This raises a natural question: can large language models (LLMs) reliably perform this kind of architectural reasoning? We find that they cannot. While LLMs produce plausible configurations, they frequently miss critical constraints, encode incorrect assumptions, and exhibit ``stickiness'' to familiar patterns. A natural workaround--iterative validation via simulation or experimentation--is often prohibitively expensive at scale and, in many cases, infeasible, particularly when comparing hardware-dependent alternatives.
  Motivated by this gap, we present Kepler, a lightweight reasoning framework for architecture design that combines structured, expert-driven specifications with SMT-based optimization. Kepler encodes architecturally significant properties--requirements, incompatibilities, and qualitative trade-offs--about systems, hardware, and workloads as constraints, and synthesizes feasible designs that optimize user-defined objectives. It operates at an abstract level, capturing ``rules-of-thumb'' rather than detailed system behavior, enabling tractable reasoning while preserving key interactions, and provides explanations for its decisions. Through experiments and case studies, we show that Kepler uncovers interactions missed by LLMs and supports systematic, explainable design exploration.

</details>


### [59] [Design Insights into Partition Placement and Routing for DNN Inference in Multi-Hop Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25571)
*Jinkun Zhang,Poonam Yadav*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对多跳边缘网络中的DNN推理，联合优化分区放置与路由，提出了一种考虑拥塞的交替优化框架，有效降低了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多跳边缘网络中执行分区DNN推理时，分区放置与流量路由相互耦合。由于原始输入、中间特征和最终输出的大小差异显著，且候选节点的计算能力各异，加之通信和计算延迟会随负载变化呈现拥塞依赖性，现有方法难以有效应对这些挑战。

Method: 针对固定分区的DNN推理，考虑少量分区且每个分区仅放置在一个节点上（无副本），构建了一个拥塞感知的混合离散-连续优化问题，同时建模路由和执行成本。提出了一种实用的交替优化框架，将分区放置与拥塞感知转发更新相结合进行求解。

Result: 在分层、规则、合成不规则及真实骨干拓扑上的数值评估表明：在IoT-边缘-云场景中，分割灵活性尤为重要；随着负载增加，拥塞感知优化的优势愈发明显；最佳操作点取决于通信与计算之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在异构多跳边缘网络中，联合考虑分区放置与拥塞感知路由对于优化DNN推理性能至关重要，为边缘智能服务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Partitioned DNN inference is a promising approach for latency-sensitive intelligent services in edge networks, since it allows different parts of a model to be executed across end devices, edge servers, and the cloud. However, in a multi-hop edge network, partition placement and inference traffic routing are inherently coupled: raw inputs, intermediate features, and final outputs may have very different sizes, while candidate nodes also differ in computation capability. In addition, both communication and computation delays can become congestion-dependent under load. In this paper, we study joint partition placement and routing for fixed-partition DNN inference over heterogeneous multi-hop edge networks. We consider a small number of DNN partitions, each placed at exactly one node without replication, and formulate a congestion-aware mixed discrete--continuous optimization problem that captures both routing and execution costs. To solve it, we develop a practical alternating framework that couples partition placement with congestion-aware forwarding updates. Through numerical evaluation on hierarchical, regular, synthetic irregular, and real backbone-inspired topologies, we show that split flexibility is particularly important in IoT--edge--cloud settings, while congestion-aware refinement becomes increasingly beneficial as the offered load grows. We further illustrate how the preferred operating point depends on the communication--computation tradeoff.

</details>


### [60] [Chorusing Synchronization Signals for Ambient 5G Backscatter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25641)
*Yunyun Feng,Chenhong Cao,Si Chen,Wei Gong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向5G反向散射通信的低功耗、高精度同步方法（SD-Sync），利用5G主同步信号包络的镜像对称特性，实现了比现有方法低30-181倍的资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G反向散射通信作为一种新兴的物联网连接方案，同步至关重要。现有基于自相关和互相关的同步方法资源消耗大，且传统互相关方法因5G多模板特性而失效，因此亟需一种兼顾高精度与低功耗的同步策略。

Method: 观察到5G主同步信号（PSS）包络具有独特的镜像对称特性，提出基于对称差分（SD）的同步方法，利用差分技术实现低功耗的PSS检测。

Result: 在包含反向散射硬件、SDR gNodeB和用户设备的测试平台上验证，SD方法仅消耗3,175个D触发器，资源消耗比NR精细定时（NFT）低87倍，比基于对称的半模板同步（SST）低181倍，比对称自相关（SA）同步低30倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的SD-Sync方法成功实现了5G反向散射通信中的高精度、低功耗同步，显著降低了硬件资源消耗，为无线反向散射通信提供了一种高效的同步解决方案。

Abstract: 5G backscatter communication presents an emerging energy-efficient IoT connectivity solution with enhanced availability and data rate advantages over traditional wireless networks. For 5G backscatter, synchronization is crucial as it ensures high-quality transmission. Popular synchronization methods employ autocorrelation and cross-correlation for accurate timing, yet they are constrained by resources. Traditional cross-correlation-based methods for resource utilization optimization also fail in 5G backscatter due to the presence of multiple templates for 5G. A synchronization strategy that supports high accuracy and low power would be highly attractive for wireless backscatter communication. We propose Symmetric Differential (SD)-based Sync, an accurate and resource-efficient synchronization method for 5G backscatter. We have observed that the envelope of the 5G Primary Synchronization Signal (PSS) exhibits a unique mirror symmetry, which enables us to employ differential techniques for low-power PSS detection. We extensively evaluated our design using a testbed of backscatter hardware, SDR gNodeB, and User Equipment (UE). Results show that our SD consumes 3,175 D flip-flops, which is 87x lower than NR fine timing (NFT), 181x lower than symmetry-based semi-template sync (SST), and 30x lower than symmetric autocorrelation (SA)-based sync.

</details>


### [61] [EOS-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25782)
*Qian Yin,Jiaxing Li,Jiaqi Cheng,Qizhang Luo,Annalisa Riccardi,Abhijit Chatterjee,Rafael Vazquez,Carlo Novara,Michalis Mavrovouniotis,Ponnuthurai Nagaratnam Suganthan,Shengzhou Bai,Xiaoxuan Hu,Lining Xing,Ming Xu,Shuang Li,Zixuan Zheng,Xin Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Yi Gu,Yanjie Song,Witold Pedrycz,Evan L. Kramer,Laio Oriel Seman,Cletah Shoko,Guohua Wu,Xinwei Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EOS-Bench，一个用于地球观测卫星成像调度问题评估的统一开源基准框架，包含1390个场景和13900个基准实例，支持跨尺度和条件的算法性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代敏捷地球观测卫星虽然提高了操作灵活性，但也显著增加了调度复杂性。由于缺乏统一的、开源的基准，难以在不同研究之间比较算法性能，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了一个集成高保真轨道动力学和平台约束的综合框架EOS-Bench，生成了1390个场景和13900个基准实例（规模从验证案例到涉及1000颗卫星和10000个请求的大型协调问题）。提出了场景特征化方案来量化结构难度（包括机会密度、任务灵活性、冲突强度和卫星拥塞等因素），并引入了多维评估协议，从任务收益、完成率、负载平衡、时效性和运行时间五个指标进行评估。

Result: 通过混合整数规划、启发式、元启发式和深度强化学习等多种方法在敏捷和非敏捷设置下进行评估，结果表明EOS-Bench能有效区分不同尺度和条件下的求解器性能，揭示了求解质量与计算效率之间的权衡关系，并提供了对场景复杂性的深入理解。

Conclusion: EOS-Bench为推进地球观测卫星调度研究提供了一个统一、可扩展的开源测试平台，有助于标准化算法比较和促进领域发展。代码和数据已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Earth observation satellite imaging scheduling is a challenging NP-hard combinatorial optimisation problem central to space mission operations. While next-generation agile Earth observation satellites (EOS) increase operational flexibility, they also significantly raise scheduling complexity. The lack of a unified, open-source benchmark makes it difficult to compare algorithms across studies. This paper introduces EOS-Bench, a comprehensive framework for systematic and reproducible evaluation of scheduling methods. By integrating high-fidelity orbital dynamics and platform constraints, EOS-Bench generates 1,390 scenarios and 13,900 benchmark instances, spanning from small-scale validation cases to large coordination problems with up to 1,000 satellites and 10,000 requests.
  We further propose a scenario characterisation scheme to quantify structural difficulty based on factors such as opportunity density, task flexibility, conflict intensity, and satellite congestion. A multidimensional evaluation protocol is introduced, assessing performance across five metrics: task profit, completion rate, workload balance, timeliness, and runtime. The framework is evaluated using mixed-integer programming, heuristics, meta-heuristics, and deep reinforcement learning across both agile and non-agile settings. Results show that EOS-Bench effectively distinguishes solver performance across scales and conditions, revealing trade-offs between solution quality and computational efficiency, and providing deeper insight into scenario complexity.
  EOS-Bench offers a unified and extensible open testbed for advancing research in Earth observation satellite scheduling. The code and data are available at https://github.com/Ethan19YQ/EOS-Bench.

</details>


### [62] [Slice Agent: Identifying and Isolating Slices in Shared Open Radio Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25857)
*Felipe Arnholda,Flavio Rocha,Lucio Prade,Cristiano Bonato Both*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对O-RAN架构中共享O-RU的上行切片识别难题，提出了一种嵌入式切片代理，通过流水线架构在2个时钟周期内完成数据包处理，支持每时隙高达3822个切片，实现了超低延迟的上行数据切片隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络切片即服务(NSaaS)是B5G/6G网络的关键使能技术，但在Open RAN架构中，共享O-RU的上行切片识别面临根本性挑战：由于O-DU负责调度，O-RU本身无法识别上行数据所属切片，在MP2MP前传场景下这一问题更加突出，需要满足同步与时序约束的超低延迟处理要求。

Method: 提出一种嵌入在O-RU中的切片代理，采用流水线架构将上行数据识别并隔离为切片特定的eCPRI数据包；设计专用路径处理时敏与灵活切片需求，实现切片隔离与优先级保障；在FPGA平台实现并验证。

Result: FPGA实现结果表明，切片代理可在2个时钟周期内完成每个数据包处理，支持每时隙高达3822个切片；实验验证了该方案的可行性、可扩展性以及在B5G/6G Open RAN部署中的高性能实时切片感知上行处理能力。

Conclusion: 该方案有效解决了共享O-RU场景下上行切片识别难题，为B5G/6G网络中原生支持端到端切片感知服务提供了切实可行的技术路径，具备良好的可扩展性和实时性能。

Abstract: Network Slice as a Service (NSaaS) is a key enabler of Beyond Fifth Generation (5G) and Sixth Generation (6G) networks, supporting next-generation applications such as extended reality (XR), immersive services, and the tactile Internet. These networks must provide native support for slice-aware services across the entire Radio Access Network (RAN), including the Radio Unit (RU), Distributed Unit (DU), Central Unit (CU), and transport segments (fronthaul, midhaul, and backhaul). However, uplink slicing identification in shared Open-RUs (O-RUs) presents a fundamental challenge because the Open-DU (O-DU) handles scheduling, and the O-RU does not inherently know which uplink data belongs to which slice. In MultiPoint-to-MultiPoint (MP2MP) fronthaul scenarios, this limitation is further exacerbated by synchronization and timing constraints, which necessitate that the O-RU process control messages and the encapsulated data be delivered with ultra-low latency. To address this issue, we propose a slicing agent embedded in the O-RU that identifies slices and segregates uplink data into slice-specific enhanced Common Public Radio Interface (eCPRI) packets. Our design employs a pipeline architecture with dedicated paths for time-sensitive, flexible slicing, enabling slice isolation and prioritization. When implemented on an Field-Programmable Gate Array (FPGA), the agent processes each packet in 2 clock cycles, supporting up to 3822 slices per slot. Experimental results validate the approach, showing its feasibility, scalability, and high-performance capabilities for real-time, slice-aware uplink processing in Beyond 5G and 6G Open RAN deployments.

</details>


### [63] [Decoding Delay Guarantees of Space Regulated Multiple Access Random Wireless Networks using Successive Interference Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25868)
*Kevin Zagalo,Jean-Marie Gorce,François Baccelli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究无蜂窝网络上行链路中采用串行干扰消除技术时的最坏传输解码延迟，利用空间网络演算（特别是空间调节）理论量化了解码延迟保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线网络中，消息需要在给定的信干噪比阈值下成功解码，且必须在严格的时间约束内完成。本文旨在解决无蜂窝网络场景下解码延迟保障的量化问题。

Method: 采用近年来提出的空间网络演算工具，特别是空间调节理论，对无蜂窝网络上行链路中采用串行干扰消除技术的情况进行分析。

Result: 成功量化了无蜂窝网络上行链路中使用串行干扰消除时的最坏传输解码延迟，证明了如何通过空间调节获得解码延迟保障。

Conclusion: 空间网络演算特别是空间调节理论是分析无线网络解码延迟保障的有效工具，为无蜂窝网络的延迟性能分析提供了理论框架和方法。

Abstract: This paper is focused on decoding delay guarantees in wireless networks, where messages have a given signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio threshold $η_0$ to meet in order to be successfully decoded, and where this should occur within some strict time constraints. Its main contribution consists in quantifying the worst-case transmissions decoding delays in the uplink of a cell-free network using successive interference cancellation. We show how such decoding delay guarantees can be obtained using spatial network calculus, a new tool introduced recently, and in particular spatial regulation.

</details>
