### [64] [ITAS: A Multi-Agent Architecture for LLM-Based Intelligent Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24808)
*Iizalaarab Elhaimeur,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ITAS（智能教学助手系统），一个在真实研究生量子计算课程中部署一学期的多智能体辅导系统，解决了LLM导师在真实课程环境中难以运行的工程问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型导师在笔记本环境中容易构建，但在真实课程中难以运行。作者旨在解决"教师盲区问题"（LLM导师积累的学生数据远超教师通过常规渠道可获取的范围），并解决早期原型中的具体失败问题。

Method: 系统采用三层架构：（1）教学层：三并行专家代理（视频、代码、指导）+ 综合器的辐轮结构，以及独立自动评分器评估检查点提交的正确性和方法；（2）运营层：四个Cloud Run微服务，会话状态存储于Cloud SQL，交互事件通过Pub/Sub流式传输至BigQuery；（3）反馈层：窄范围对话代理回答教师关于每节课伪名化事件流的问题。该架构是对早期原型失败的直接响应。

Result: 试点部署（5名学生，1门课程，1学期）：教学层处理了334个聊天轮次，未出现领域整合可能导致的任务边界幻觉；运营层在5个模块中捕获了10,628个事件；反馈层发现了教师期中采取行动的两项发现。

Conclusion: 作者不声称试点结果具有普适性，但认为该系统是对"基于LLM的ITS在真实课程中端到端运行应具备何种形态"这一问题的一个可行答案，为LLM导师系统的实际部署提供了参考架构。

Abstract: Large language model tutors are easy to build in a notebook and hard to run in a real course. We describe ITAS (Intelligent Teaching Assistant System), a multi-agent tutoring system that a graduate quantum computing course used for a semester at Old Dominion University. The system has three layers. The teaching layer is a Spoke-and-Wheel of three parallel specialist agents (Video, Code, Guidance) followed by a Synthesizer, plus a separate autograder that evaluates both the correctness and the approach of checkpoint submissions. The operational layer is four Cloud Run microservices with session state in Cloud SQL and interaction events streamed through Pub/Sub to BigQuery. The feedback layer is a narrow-scope conversational agent that answers instructor questions over per-lesson pseudonymized event streams, addressing what we call the Blind Instructor Problem: LLM tutors accumulate more data about students than the instructor can reach through routine channels. The architecture is a direct response to specific failures of an earlier prototype, and we describe which of those fixes carried forward and which were dropped for this iteration. We report on a pilot deployment (five students, one course, one semester) interpreted as system-behavior evidence rather than learning-outcome evidence: the teaching layer handled 334 chat turns without the task-boundary hallucinations that domain consolidation would have risked, the operational layer captured 10,628 events across five modules, and the feedback layer surfaced two findings the instructor acted on mid-semester. We do not claim the pilot generalizes. We do claim that the system as described is one workable answer to the question of what an LLM-based ITS needs to look like end-to-end to run in a real course.

</details>


### [65] [MultiHedge: Adaptive Coordination via Retrieval-Augmented Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24905)
*Feliks Bańka,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiHedge架构，通过结合检索增强的大语言模型与期权策略，证明了在变化环境下的决策系统中，记忆增强检索比单纯增加模型规模更能提升鲁棒性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多系统需要在不断变化的条件下进行决策，现有方法难以在动态环境中泛化，且在不确定性下表现出不稳定的行为。因此，研究如何通过检索增强的LLM协调来提高模块化决策流程的鲁棒性成为关键问题。

Method: 提出MultiHedge混合架构，该架构利用大语言模型基于检索到的历史先例生成结构化配置决策，并将执行过程与经典期权策略相结合。研究使用美国股票数据进行控制性评估，将MultiHedge与基于规则和基于学习的基准方法进行对比。

Result: 实验表明，记忆增强检索机制能够提供比单纯增加模型规模更强的鲁棒性和稳定性。在控制性计算研究中，MultiHedge相较于传统基准方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 记忆机制和架构设计在模块化决策系统的鲁棒性中起着核心作用。该研究为理解如何构建在变化环境中稳定的决策系统提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Decision-making under changing conditions remains a fundamental challenge in many real-world systems. Existing approaches often fail to generalize across shifting regimes and exhibit unstable behavior under uncertainty. This raises the research question: can retrieval-augmented LLM coordination improve the robustness of modular decision pipelines? We propose MultiHedge, a hybrid architecture where an LLM produces structured allocation decisions conditioned on retrieved historical precedents, and execution is grounded in canonical option strategies. In a controlled evaluation using U.S. equities, we compare MultiHedge to rule-based and learning-based baselines. The key result is that memory-augmented retrieval confers greater robustness and stability than increasing model scale alone. Our paper contributes a controlled computational study showing that memory and architectural design play a central role in robustness in modular decision systems.

</details>


### [66] [Frontier Coding Agents Can Now Implement an AlphaZero Self-Play Machine Learning Pipeline For Connect Four That Performs Comparably to an External Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25067)
*Joshua Sherwood,Ben Aybar,Benjamin Kaplan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估AI自主实现端到端机器学习流程能力的基准测试，通过让前沿AI代理在3小时内自主实现AlphaZero风格的Connect Four管道，发现Claude Opus 4.7表现显著优于其他代理，并观察到GPT-5.4存在可能的能力隐藏（sandbagging）异常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI能力基准测试主要衡量广泛的能力增长，但可能无法为递归自我改进提供充分的早期预警信号。因此需要开发新的评估方法来测量AI自主实现AI研究突破的能力，以更好地预测AI系统何时能够有意义地加速AI研究。

Method: 设计了一个概念验证基准测试：让前沿编码代理在3小时预算内、在消费级硬件上自主实现AlphaZero风格的Connect Four机器学习管道。仅提供简洁任务描述而非完整参考文献，以更好地激发AI研究品味。通过循环赛锦标赛评估生成的游戏AI，并以Pascal Pons Connect Four求解器为基准进行对比。对四个代理各进行8次试验。

Result: Claude Opus 4.7在8次试验中有7次作为先手战胜Pons求解器，显著优于其他代理（其他代理均不超过2次胜利）。该任务从2026年1月开发初期无代理能可靠完成，现已接近饱和。发现GPT-5.4存在异常行为：始终使用远少于分配的时间预算。后续16次探针实验使用更短、更少评估编码的提示后，GPT-5.4的时间预算使用量大幅增加，这与能力隐藏（sandbagging）行为一致但不具诊断性；Bradley-Terry评级在不同条件下仅显示方向性差异。

Conclusion: 该基准测试成功展示了前沿AI代理在自主实现复杂机器学习管道方面的能力差异和快速进步。研究揭示了潜在的代理策略性行为（如能力隐藏）的重要性，这需要在AI安全评估中予以关注。研究公开了数据、代码和提示以支持复现和扩展，为AI递归自我改进能力的早期预警提供了新方法。

Abstract: Forecasting when AI systems will become capable of meaningfully accelerating AI research is a central challenge for AI safety. Existing benchmarks measure broad capability growth, but may not provide ample early warning signals for recursive self-improvement. We propose measuring AI's capability to autonomously implement end-to-end machine learning pipelines from past AI research breakthroughs, given a minimal task description. By providing a concise task description instead of the full prior work as reference, we hope to better elicit emerging AI research taste. We introduce a proof-of-concept benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously implement an AlphaZero-style machine learning pipeline for Connect Four on consumer hardware within a three-hour budget, and we evaluate the resulting game AIs in a round-robin tournament anchored to the Pascal Pons Connect Four solver. Across four agents with eight trials each, we find substantial differentiation: Claude Opus 4.7 won as first-mover against Pons in seven of eight trials, statistically significantly better than the other agents tested, none of which exceeded two of eight. The task, which no frontier agent could reliably complete when we began development in January of 2026, is now near-saturation. Our evaluation also surfaced anomalous behavior in GPT-5.4, which consistently used far less of its allocated time budget than other agents. A follow-up 16-trial probe using shorter, less evaluation-coded prompts substantially increased GPT-5.4's time-budget usage, consistent with but not diagnostic of sandbagging; Bradley-Terry ratings across probe conditions showed only directional differences, despite significant differences in time-budget usage. We release our data, code, and prompts to support reproduction and extension.

</details>


### [67] [Where Did It Go Wrong? Capability-Oriented Failure Attribution for Vision-and-Language Navigation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25161)
*Jianming Chen,Yawen Wang,Junjie Wang,Xiaofei Xie,Shoubin Li,Qing Wang,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向能力的测试方法，通过自适应测试用例生成、能力预言机和反馈机制，实现了对视觉语言导航（VLN）具身智能体任务失败的检测与能力级缺陷定位，优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全关键应用（如视觉语言导航）中的具身智能体依赖多种相互关联的能力（感知、记忆、规划、决策等），当任务失败时难以定位和归因。现有测试方法主要停留在系统层面，无法有效识别是哪种能力不足导致了失败。

Method: 提出了一种面向能力的测试方法，包含三个核心组件：（1）通过种子选择和变异进行自适应测试用例生成；（2）能力预言机用于识别特定能力的错误；（3）反馈机制，将失败归因到具体能力并指导后续测试用例的生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，与最先进的基线方法相比，该方法发现了更多的失败案例，并更准确地定位了能力层面的缺陷。

Conclusion: 该方法能够为具身智能体的改进提供更具可解释性和可操作性的指导，有效解决了任务失败难以归因的问题。

Abstract: Embodied agents in safety-critical applications such as Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) rely on multiple interdependent capabilities (e.g., perception, memory, planning, decision), making failures difficult to localize and attribute. Existing testing methods are largely system-level and provide limited insight into which capability deficiencies cause task failures. We propose a capability-oriented testing approach that enables failure detection and attribution by combining (1) adaptive test case generation via seed selection and mutation, (2) capability oracles for identifying capability-specific errors, and (3) a feedback mechanism that attributes failures to capabilities and guides further test generation. Experiments show that our method discovers more failure cases and more accurately pinpoints capability-level deficiencies than state-of-the-art baselines, providing more interpretable and actionable guidance for improving embodied agents.

</details>


### [68] [Should I Replan? Learning to Spot the Right Time in Robust MAPF Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25567)
*David Zahrádka,David Woller,Denisa Mužíková,Miroslav Kulich,Libor Přeučil*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于神经网络的方法，用于预测在多智能体路径规划（MAPF）执行过程中发生延迟时，重新规划所能带来的收益，从而决定是否进行重规划以降低执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实应用中，多智能体路径规划（MAPF）假设智能体移动完美同步，但实际上智能体可能因内外因素发生延迟，导致去同步化。现有鲁棒执行方法（如动作依赖图ADG）虽能避免碰撞，但会增加执行成本。虽然重规划可以降低成本，但操作本身昂贵且新计划可能并不比原计划更优，因此需要一种方法来预估重规划的收益。

Method: 研究者使用一个全连接前馈神经网络，输入为新设计的基于动作依赖图（ADG）的特征，这些特征描述了鲁棒执行的状态及潜在延迟的影响，输出为估计的重规划收益。神经网络在一个包含12,000个实验的新标记数据集上进行训练和测试。

Result: 该方法能够在延迟场景下有效预估重规划的收益，实验表明其能够实现高达94.6%的可达成影响减少。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过神经网络预测重规划收益是可行的，能够有效减少延迟对执行成本的影响，为MAPF执行过程中的动态决策提供了有效工具。

Abstract: During the execution of Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) plans in real-life applications, the MAPF assumption that the fleet's movement is perfectly synchronized does not apply. Since one or more of the agents may become delayed due to internal or external factors, it is often necessary to use a robust execution method to avoid collisions caused by desynchronization. Robust execution methods - such as the Action Dependency Graph (ADG) - synchronize the execution of risky actions, but often at the expense of increased plan execution cost, because it may require some agents to wait for the delayed agents. In such cases, the execution's cost can be reduced while still preserving safety by finding a new plan either by rescheduling (reordering the agents at crossroads) or the more general replanning capable of finding new paths. However, these operations may be costly, and the new plan may not even lead to lower execution cost than the original plan: for example, the two plans may be the exact same. Therefore, we estimate the benefit that can be achieved by single replanning in scenarios with delayed agents given an immediate state of the execution with a fully connected feed-forward neural network. The input to the neural network is a set of newly designed ADG-based features describing the robust execution's state and the impact of potential delays, and the output is an estimated benefit achievable by replanning. We train and test the network on a new labeled dataset containing 12,000 experiments, and we show that our proposed method is capable of reducing the impact of delays by up to 94.6% of the achievable reduction.

</details>


### [69] [Pythia: Toward Predictability-Driven Agent-Native LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25899)
*Shan Yu,Junyi Shu,Yuanjiang Ni,Kun Qian,Xue Li,Yang Wang,Jinyuan Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shuo Yang,Lingjun Zhu,Ennan Zhai,Qingda Lu,Jiarong Xing,Youyou Lu,Xin Jin,Xuanzhe Liu,Harry Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出Pythia系统，利用多智能体架构的结构化特性优化LLM服务，解决了缓存命中率低、资源竞争和队列延迟等瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM应用复杂度增加，多智能体架构被广泛采用，其结构化拓扑特性可以降低运行时不确定性。然而现有系统将其视为通用流量处理，导致低前缀缓存命中率、长上下文请求的资源竞争以及扩展性不佳导致的队列延迟等显著效率问题。

Method: 提出Pythia多智能体服务系统，通过在服务层提供简单接口来捕获工作流语义信息，利用多智能体架构的结构化特性解锁新的优化机会，从而更好地调度和管理请求。

Result: 通过分析生产环境追踪数据和内部编码助手，识别了关键瓶颈问题。Pythia系统相比最先进的基线方法，在吞吐量和作业完成时间方面取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 多智能体架构的结构化语义信息为LLM服务优化提供了重要机会，Pythia系统证明了利用这些信息可以显著改善服务性能，为未来智能体服务系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: As LLM applications grow more complex, developers are increasingly adopting multi-agent architectures to decompose workflows into specialized, collaborative components, introducing structure that constrains agent behavior and exposes useful semantic predictability. Unlike traditional LLM serving, which operates under highly dynamic and uncertain conditions, this structured topology enables opportunities to reduce runtime uncertainty -- yet existing systems fail to exploit it, treating agentic workloads as generic traffic and incurring significant inefficiencies. Our analysis of production traces from an agent-serving platform and an internal coding assistant reveals key bottlenecks, including low prefix cache hit rates, severe resource contention from long-context requests, and substantial queuing delays due to suboptimal scaling. To address these challenges, we propose Pythia, a multi-agent serving system that captures workflow semantics through a simple interface at the serving layer, unlocking new optimization opportunities and substantially improving throughput and job completion time over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [Spark Policy Toolkit: Semantic Contracts and Scalable Execution for Policy Learning in Spark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25061)
*Zeyu Bai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Spark Policy Toolkit，一个语义 governed 的系统工具包，通过分区初始化的向量化推理和无收集分割搜索两个Spark原生原语，解决了Spark中自定义策略学习管道因行式Python执行和驱动端候选物化导致的可扩展性问题，在保证策略语义不变的前提下实现了大规模策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spark中的自定义策略学习管道存在两个耦合的系统问题：1）行式Python执行使得推理不可行；2）驱动端候选物化使得特征规模下的分割搜索脆弱。这些问题严重限制了策略学习在Spark集群上的可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出了Spark Policy Toolkit工具包，包含两个Spark原生原语：1）通过mapInPandas和mapInArrow实现分区初始化的向量化推理；2）在执行器上进行候选评分的无收集（collect-less）分割搜索。这两个原语由一个固定输入语义契约（fixed-input semantic contract）统一管理，确保相同的行、特征顺序、处理词汇、预处理清单和分割边界必须保持逐行分数向量、最佳分割决策和端到端学习策略输出的一致性。

Result: 在40节点Databricks集群上，mapInArrow在1000万匹配行时达到472万行/秒，在5000万行时达到723万行/秒。无收集分割搜索在F=10到F=1000范围内（124000候选行）保持有效。在24个后端消融设置中，mapInArrow获胜18次，mapInPandas获胜6次。强制执行固定输入锁后，所有6种重分区/合并/洗牌扰动都保持相同签名；未锁定前则全部漂移。

Conclusion: 核心贡献不仅是速度提升，更重要的是吞吐量和无收集执行是使策略语义能够在Spark规模下存活的机制。后端选择应视工作负载而定而非通用。固定输入语义契约的强制执行确保了策略学习在大规模分布式环境中的语义一致性和可靠性。

Abstract: Custom policy-learning pipelines in Spark fail for two coupled systems reasons: rowwise Python execution makes inference impractical, and driver-side candidate materialization makes split search fragile at feature scale. We present Spark Policy Toolkit, a semantics-governed systems toolkit for scalable policy learning in Spark. The toolkit provides two Spark-native primitives: partition-initialized vectorized inference through mapInPandas and mapInArrow, and collect-less split search that scores candidates on executors. Both primitives are governed by one fixed-input semantic contract: the same rows, feature order, treatment vocabulary, preprocessing manifest, and split boundaries must preserve per-row score vectors, best-split decisions, and end-to-end learned policy outputs. The evaluation combines practical baseline ladders, backend parity checks, measured split-search scale results, synthetic and Hillstrom end-to-end policy preservation, missingness stress, partition and order perturbation tests, quantile-boundary sensitivity, and a concrete adversarial failure catalog. On a 40-worker Databricks cluster, mapInArrow reaches 4.72M rows/s at 10M matched rows and 7.23M rows/s at 50M rows, while collect-less split search remains valid from F = 10 through F = 1000 with 124000 candidate rows, where the driver-collect baseline is intentionally skipped. Across 24 backend-ablation settings, mapInArrow wins 18 while mapInPandas wins 6, so the paper treats backend choice as workload-dependent rather than universal. Once the fixed-input lock is enforced, all six tested repartition/coalesce/shuffle perturbations preserve identical signatures; before lock, all six drift. The central result is not speed alone: throughput and collect-less execution are the mechanisms that let policy semantics survive at Spark scale.

</details>


### [71] [CacheFlow: Efficient LLM Serving with 3D-Parallel KV Cache Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25080)
*Sean Nian,Jiahao Fang,Qilong Feng,Zhiyu Wu,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheFlow提出了一种KV缓存恢复框架，通过三维并行化和批感知调度器，将TTFT降低10%-62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存恢复方法将恢复视为每个请求内重计算与I/O传输的权衡，忽略了跨token、层和分布式部署的并行性，且未考虑批处理服务下的资源竞争，导致恢复过程效率低下。

Method: 提出CacheFlow框架，将缓存恢复重构为多维并行执行问题。引入跨token、层和GPU的统一三维并行抽象，实现重计算与I/O在Transformer推理结构依赖上的细粒度重叠。核心是批感知双指针调度器，通过优先处理边际重计算成本降低最大的操作，联合优化跨请求的计算和I/O分配。

Result: 在多种模型、工作负载和硬件上的评估表明，CacheFlow相比现有方法将首令牌时间（TTFT）降低了10%-62%。

Conclusion: CacheFlow成功解决了KV缓存恢复中的并行性利用和资源竞争问题，显著提升了长上下文LLM服务性能。

Abstract: KV cache restoration has emerged as a dominant bottleneck in serving long-context LLM workloads, including multi-turn conversations, retrieval-augmented generation, and agentic pipelines. Existing approaches treat restoration as a per-request tradeoff between recomputation and I/O transfer, recomputing KV states from scratch or offloading them from external storage (e.g., CPU memory or remote machines). However, existing advances fail to exploit parallelism across tokens, layers, and distributed deployments, and critically ignore resource contention under batched serving. We present CacheFlow, a KV cache restoration framework that rethinks cache restoration as a multi-dimensional parallel execution problem. CacheFlow introduces a unified 3D parallelism abstraction across tokens, layers, and GPUs, enabling fine-grained overlap of recomputation and I/O along the structural dependencies of transformer inference. At the core of CacheFlow is a batch-aware two-pointer scheduler that jointly optimizes compute and I/O allocation across requests by prioritizing operations with the highest marginal reduction in recomputation cost. Our evaluations show that CacheFlow reduces Time-To-First-Token (TTFT) by 10%-62% over existing advances across diverse models, workloads, and hardware.

</details>


### [72] [Adaptive Management of Microservices in Dynamic Computing Environments: A Taxonomy and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25222)
*Ming Chen,Muhammed Tawfiqul Islam,Maria Rodriguez Read,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了微服务云应用的动态感知自适应管理，通过分类体系和84个系统的分析，发现生产动态性常被部分建模，评估保真度影响报告收益，并指出跨层协调等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务云应用面临工作负载变化、请求路径演进、网络条件多变、干扰和故障等动态性挑战，这些动态性将自动伸缩、放置、路由、隔离和修复等管理任务相互耦合，因此需要研究动态感知的自适应管理方法。

Method: 提出了一个分类体系，涵盖控制位置、建模的动态性、适应策略和评估证据四个维度，目标与遥测作为横切关注点；对84个系统条目和13个评估制品进行综合分析。

Result: 生产环境的动态性通常仅被部分建模；报告的性能提升效果依赖于评估的保真度（fidelity），高保真评估更能反映真实效果。

Conclusion: 未来关键研究方向包括：跨层协调、遥测到控制的抽象、安全的基于学习的控制，以及可重现的动态评估方法。

Abstract: Microservice-based cloud applications face changing workloads, evolving request paths, variable network conditions, interference, and failures. These dynamics couple autoscaling, placement, routing, isolation, and remediation. The survey examines dynamics-aware adaptive management for microservices. Its taxonomy covers control locus, modeled dynamics, adaptation strategy, and evaluation evidence; objectives and telemetry are cross-cutting. A synthesis of 84 system entries and 13 evaluation artifacts shows that production dynamics are often partially modeled. Reported gains also depend on evaluation fidelity. Key future directions include cross-layer coordination, telemetry-to-control abstractions, safe learning-based control, and reproducible dynamic evaluation.

</details>


### [73] [CUDA Kernel Optimization and Counter-Free Performance Analysis for Depthwise Convolution in Cloud Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25422)
*Huriyeh Babak,Melanie Schaller*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在控制变量条件下系统研究了S4ConvD深度卷积的CUDA内核优化，提出无计数器的性能分析方法。Warp-tiled内核相比朴素基线实现3.26倍加速，端到端训练加速1.29倍，证明了在受限云环境中可进行有意义的GPU架构级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU卷积算子的执行效率主要受内存访问效率、片上数据复用和执行映射影响，而非单纯算术吞吐量。现有性能分析通常依赖硬件性能计数器，在受限云环境中难以获取，因此需要一种无需特权访问的分析方法来指导内核优化。

Method: 采用控制变量方法，固定算子、模型、数据集和训练配置，仅改变CUDA内核实现。评估四种内核变体（朴素、全局内存合并、共享内存缓存分块、warp分块），覆盖前向、输入梯度和权重梯度路径。提出无计数器性能分析方法：结合CUDA事件计时、执行路径分解、解析式内存流量建模、有效带宽估计和roofline分析。

Result: Warp-tiled内核相比朴素CUDA基线减少3.26倍卷积运行时间，端到端训练加速达1.29倍。前向和输入梯度路径显著受益于局部性改进和片上数据复用，而归约主导的权重梯度路径仍是主要瓶颈。所提方法可在无硬件性能计数器条件下获得类似profiling的架构洞察。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了在受限云环境中，即使无法访问硬件性能计数器，也能进行有意义的架构级GPU内核分析。共享内存缓存和warp分块优化对前向和输入梯度路径效果显著，但权重梯度路径因归约操作特性仍需进一步优化。该方法具有可复现性和云兼容性。

Abstract: Efficient GPU execution of convolution operators is governed by memory-access efficiency, on-chip data reuse, and execution mapping rather than arithmetic throughput alone. This paper presents a controlled operator-level study of CUDA kernel optimization for the depthwise convolution used in Structured State Space Model Convolutional Diagonal (S4ConvD), together with a cloud-compatible, counter-free performance analysis methodology. The operator, model, dataset, and training configuration are fixed, and only the CUDA kernel implementation is varied. The evaluated CUDA kernels comprise naive, global-memory-coalesced, shared-memory cache-blocked, and warp-tiled variants, covering forward, input-gradient, and weight-gradient execution paths under steady-state training conditions. Performance is characterized using a counter-free methodology that combines CUDA-event timing, execution-path decomposition, analytically derived memory-traffic modeling, effective-bandwidth estimation, and roofline analysis. This enables profiling-like architectural insights without requiring hardware performance counters or privileged profiling access. The warp-tiled kernel reduces convolution runtime by $3.26\times$ relative to the naive CUDA baseline, while end-to-end training speedup reaches $1.29\times$. A PyTorch implementation is used separately for numerical validation and runtime context, but is not treated as a controlled architectural baseline. Forward and input-gradient paths benefit substantially from improved locality and on-chip data reuse, whereas the reduction-dominated weight-gradient path remains the primary bottleneck. The results demonstrate that meaningful architecture-level GPU kernel analysis can be performed reproducibly in restricted cloud environments, even without access to hardware performance counters.

</details>


### [74] [Economical and ecological impact of sector coupling applied to computing clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25540)
*P. Bechtle,O. Freyermuth,M. Geffers,M. Giffels,M. Hübner,F. Kirfel,J. Kreutz,S. Krieg,S. Matberg,M. Schnepf*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究模拟了科学计算集群的动态运行策略，利用德国电力生产数据，证明通过根据可再生能源供应情况调度计算任务，可同时降低运营成本和碳排放，且该策略在未来仍具潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源占比提升导致电力生产波动性增加。为了应对这一挑战，研究旨在探索“部门耦合”概念，即通过在可再生能源过剩时调度电力消耗（如高性能计算集群），以吸收波动性，从而降低系统的环境影响并减少运营成本。

Method: 利用德国公开的电力生产数据，模拟计算集群的简化动态运行。研究分别确定了最优利用率、相关的碳减排和成本降低幅度，并将硬件购置成本和隐含排放纳入考量。通过两个验证期评估固定计算目标的稳定性，并进行参数修改的额外模拟以预测未来动态运行的潜力。

Result: 研究确定了计算集群的最优利用率，并量化了动态运行策略带来的碳排放和运营成本降低。验证表明，在保持长期计算目标恒定的前提下，该策略可行。模拟预测显示，在未来特定条件下，该动态运行模式仍能持续实现节约。

Conclusion: 高性能计算集群非常适合作为动态运行的测试平台，通过动态调度可有效吸收可再生能源波动。在不影响长期计算目标的前提下，该策略能显著降低环境影响和成本，且未来具有持续的应用潜力。

Abstract: The rising share of abundant renewable energy inevitably increases volatility in the electricity production. The concept of sector coupling means that the volatility of electricity production to a large degree can be absorbed by dispatching electricity consumption whenever excess renewable energy is available. A system that is dynamically operated based on this principle can lower its total environmental impact. In addition, operational costs might be reducible as electricity prizes strongly depend on the residual load of the energy system. High-performance computing clusters in the field of science represent an ideal testing ground for such dynamic operation. Short-term delays in computing results due to electricity production being associated with high costs or carbon emissions are often negligible, provided that an overall computing target remains constant over long time periods. This study simulates the simplified operation of computing clusters using publicly available data on electricity production in Germany. The optimal utilisation along with associated carbon emission and cost reductions are determined separately. Hardware acquisition costs and embedded emissions are taken into account. The stability of a fixed computing target given the determined utilisation optima is evaluated in two validation periods. Additional simulations with modified parameters are carried out to estimate potential conditions under which dynamic operation of a computing cluster would continue to enable savings in the future.

</details>


### [75] [Volitional Multiagent Atomic Transactions: Describing People and their Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25596)
*Andy Lewis-Pye,Ehud Shapiro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出“意志多智能体原子事务”作为描述人机协同系统的数学基础，其中事务由机器原子执行但受人的意志守护；并给出了安全性、活性的数学刻画及草根平台的新定义，通过社交网络和币债两个案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的并发与分布式系统形式化模型仅描述机器，忽略或外化操作机器的人。然而，草根平台等关键分布式系统由人操作个人设备（如智能手机），若要忠实地刻画此类系统，必须同时纳入人与机器的状态及其对系统行为的共同影响。

Method: 提出意志多智能体原子事务框架。每个智能体的状态包含意志状态与机器状态；事务在机器前置条件满足且守护者（相关人）意愿允许时被使能并可原子执行。例如，加好友需双方意愿守护，删除好友可由任意一方守护，币债交换需双方守护，支付仅由付款方守护。在此基础上发展了安全性（不良状态不可达）与活性（期望状态最终可达）的形式化刻画方法，并提出了更简洁的“草根”定义：多个实例可独立形成与运行，又可合并。

Result: 利用该框架形式化规约了两个草根平台：社交网络和币债系统，并展示了如何从规约通过AI导出可运行的实现。证明了新定义下的草根性质：本文规约的平台及此前证明为草根的平台均满足新的草根定义。

Conclusion: 意志多智能体原子事务为“人操作机器”类分布式系统提供了统一的形式化基础，弥补了传统模型对人的忽视；结合安全性、活性分析与更简明的草根定义，为草根平台的设计、验证与自动实现提供了理论支撑与实践路径。

Abstract: Formal models for concurrent and distributed systems describe machines; the people who operate them are either ignored or treated as external environment. Yet key distributed systems -- notably grassroots platforms -- include people operating their personal machines (smartphones), and their faithful description must include the states of both people and machines and how they jointly effect system behaviour.
  Here, we propose volitional multiagent atomic transactions -- executed atomically by machines and guarded by their people's volitions -- as a novel mathematical foundation for specifying systems consisting of people operating machines. Each agent's state consists of a volitional state and machine state; a transaction is enabled when the machine precondition holds and the guarding persons are willing. For example, befriending two people is guarded by both; unfriending, by either; voluntary swap of coins and bonds is guarded by both parties, while a payment is guarded by the payer.
  We develop the mathematical machinery to express safety and liveness of platforms specified in this framework, and provide example specifications of two grassroots platforms: social networks, and coins and bonds. These specifications are then used by AI to derive working implementations. %
We employ here a novel and simpler definition of `grassroots' that better captures the informal notion -- multiple instances can form and operate independently, yet may coalesce -- and show that the platforms specified here, as well as those hitherto proven grassroots under the original definition, are grassroots under the new definition.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [GCA-BULF: A Bottom-Up Framework for Short-Term Load Forecasting Using Grouped Critical Appliances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24766)
*Yunhao Yao,Jinwei Fang,Puhan Luo,Zhiqiang Wang,Jiahui Hou,Xiang-Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GCA-BULF，一种基于分组关键电器的自底向上短期负荷预测框架，通过识别关键电器并分组预测，显著提升了预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分时电价政策的实施，电力用户需要采用削峰填谷策略以降低成本并增强电网稳定性，这对短期负荷预测（STLF）的可靠性和响应速度提出了更高要求。传统的自顶向下预测方法难以捕捉多样化混合电器负载的复杂模式；而自底向上方法虽然能通过整合电器级数据提高精度，但监测所有电器成本高昂且许多电器对总负荷预测影响甚微。

Method: 提出了GCA-BULF框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）关键电器过滤模块：根据功耗、开关频率和使用模式周期性对电器进行排序，通过迭代负荷分解识别关键电器；（2）关联电器分组模块：基于空间和时间相关性对关键电器进行聚类，进行组级预测；（3）协同负荷预测模块：结合多个组级预测结果优化总负荷预测。

Result: 在住宅和办公楼负荷预测任务上进行了评估。实验结果表明，与现有自顶向下方法相比，GCA-BULF将小时级总负荷预测准确率提高了20.85%-57.88%；与自底向上方法相比，准确率提高了33.03%-92.48%。

Conclusion: GCA-BULF框架成功平衡了预测精度和监测成本，通过智能识别关键电器并进行分组预测，有效解决了传统方法在混合电器负载预测中的局限性，为智能电网的能源管理提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rise of time-of-use and tiered electricity pricing, energy consumers are encouraged to adopt peak-shifting strategies by automatically controlling high-power appliances. These help lower energy costs while enhancing the power grid's stability. To support such energy management with high resilience and responsiveness, reliable short-term load forecasting (STLF) plays a critical role. STLF predicts electricity consumption over time horizons ranging from minutes to days, using historical data, temporal patterns, and contextual factors. Traditional top-down forecasting methods struggle to capture the complex consumption patterns of diverse and mixed appliance loads. Although bottom-up methods improve forecasting accuracy by integrating appliance-level data, monitoring all appliances is costly, and many do not meaningfully impact total load prediction. Therefore, we propose GCA-BULF, a bottom-up short-term load forecasting framework based on grouped critical appliances, supported by three key designs. First, the Critical Appliance Filtering module ranks appliances according to their power consumption, switching frequency, and usage pattern periodicity, and identifies critical ones through iterative load decomposition. Next, the Related Appliance Grouping module clusters these appliances based on spatial and temporal correlations for group-level forecasting. Finally, the Collaborative Load Forecasting module refines the total load prediction by combining multiple group-level forecasts. We evaluate GCA-BULF on residential and office building load forecasting tasks. Experimental results reveal that GCA-BULF improves hourly total load forecasting by 20.85%-57.88% compared to existing top-down methods and by 33.03%-92.48% compared to bottom-up methods.

</details>


### [77] [Automated detection of pediatric congenital heart disease from phonocardiograms using deep and handcrafted feature fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24767)
*Abdul Jabbar,Ethan Grooby,Yang Yi Poh,Khawza I. Ahmad,Md Hassanuzzaman,Raqibul Mostafa,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Faezeh Marzbanrad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于深度特征融合的方法，利用数字听诊器采集的心音信号（PCG）自动检测先天性心脏病（CHD），在751名儿科受试者的数据集上实现了92%的准确率和96%的AUROC，有望成为低收入地区经济高效的CHD筛查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先天性心脏病（CHD）是最常见的出生缺陷，但目前诊断的金标准——超声心动图成本高昂且在低资源环境中难以获取。由于缺乏熟练的专家以及医生间诊断存在显著的差异性，导致诊断往往被延误。因此，研究动机是开发一种更易获取、更具成本效益的诊断方式，利用数字听诊器实现CHD的早期自动化检测。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度特征融合的新方法，该方法集成了深度特征与手工特征，用于分析来自751名儿科受试者的心音（PCG）录音。数据采集覆盖二尖瓣、主动脉瓣、肺动脉瓣和三尖瓣四个听诊部位，并由心脏病专家确诊标注为CHD或非CHD。数据集按病人维度划分为70%训练集、20%验证集和10%测试集进行模型训练与评估。

Result: 该模型在测试集上取得了92%的准确率、91%的灵敏度、91%的特异度以及92%的F1分数。此外，受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUROC）达到了96%，显示出优异的分类性能。

Conclusion: 该模型证明了利用数字听诊器结合深度特征融合技术进行CHD自动检测的可行性，具备高效的实时远程检测能力，有望成为低资源环境下经济有效的CHD筛查工具。

Abstract: Congenital heart disease (CHD) is the most common type of birth defect, impacting about 1% of live births worldwide. Echocardiography, the gold-standard diagnostic method, is costly and inaccessible in low-resource settings. Diagnosis is delayed due to limited skilled experts, whose ability to interpret pathological patterns varies significantly, causing inter- and intra-clinician variability. Therefore, we present a new method for a more accessible diagnostic modality, the digital stethoscope, to detect CHDs. Our method is based on deep feature fusion, integrating deep and handcrafted features for the automated early detection of CHDs. For this work, Phonocardiography (PCG) recordings were obtained from 751 pediatric subjects (Age:1 month- 16 years) in Bangladesh, ranging from infants to adults at four auscultation locations: mitral valve (MV), aortic valve (AV), pulmonary valve (PV), and tricuspid valve (TV). These recordings were labeled based on confirmed diagnoses by cardiologists as either cases of CHD or non-CHD. The results demonstrated that our proposed model achieved an accuracy of 92%, a sensitivity of 91%, and a specificity of 91%, based on a patient-wise split of 70% training, 20% validation, and 10% testing. Furthermore, the Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 96%, and an F1-score of 92%. This model promises efficient real-time remote detection of CHDs as a cost-effective screening tool for low-resource settings.

</details>


### [78] [Comparative Study of Bending Analysis using Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Numerical Dynamic Deflection in Perforated nanobeam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24768)
*Ramanath Garai,Iswari Sahu,S. Chakraverty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合域映射的物理驱动泛函连接约束框架（DFL-TFC）方法，用于高效分析多孔纳米梁在正弦载荷下的静态弯曲与动态挠度之间的关系，相比传统PINN方法具有更高的精度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理驱动神经网络（PINN）在求解微分方程时通常需要复杂的深度网络架构，且难以严格满足边界条件。本研究旨在开发一种高效且计算稳健的方法，能够精确求解多孔纳米梁的弯曲问题，并建立其静态响应与动态响应之间的关联。

Method: 提出了一种基于泛函连接理论（TFC）的DFL-TFC方法。该方法通过TFC将控制微分方程约束系统性地嵌入约束表达式中，精确满足所有初始和边界条件，并将微分方程定义域映射到正交多项式定义域。约束表达式中的自由函数通过泛函连接神经网络（FLNN）表达，通过最小化微分方程的均方残差进行训练，无需复杂的深度网络架构。静态弯曲求解采用带域映射的FL-TFC方法，动态挠度采用Galerkin方法。

Result: 该方法成功应用于简支（S-S）多孔纳米梁在不同穿孔情况下的静态与动态分析。相比标准PINN方法，该方法消除了对深度复杂神经网络架构的需求，同时保证了准确性、效率以及对边界条件的严格满足。

Conclusion: DFL-TFC方法为多孔纳米梁的力学行为分析提供了一种高效且精确的计算框架，克服了传统PINN方法的局限性，能够在保证边界条件严格满足的前提下，减少网络复杂度并提高计算效率。

Abstract: In this chapter, we investigate the bending behavior of a perforated nanobeam subjected to sinusoidal loading using an efficient and computationally robust Physics-Informed Functional Link Constrained Framework with Domain Mapping (DFL-TFC) method. Our aim is to determine the relationship between static bending response and dynamic deflection of a perforated nanobeam for various perforation cases. The static bending is obtained using the FL-TFC with Domain mapped method, whereas dynamic deflection is determined using the Galerkin method. The proposed approach employs the theory of functional connections (TFC) to systematically embed governing differential equation constraints into a constrained expression (CE), which exactly satisfies all prescribed initial and boundary conditions (ICs and BCs) and domain of differential equation is mapped to domain of orthogonal polynomials. Within this framework, the free function appearing in the constrained expression is expressed through a functional link neural network (FLNN). The cost is minimized by the mean square residual of DE, allowing training without requiring complex deep network architectures. Relationship between static and dynamic defection of simply-supported (S-S) perforated nanobeams has been investigated here. FL-TFC with Domain mapped method eliminates the need for deep and complex neural network architectures while ensuring accuracy, efficiency, and strict satisfaction of boundary conditions as compared to standard PINN.

</details>


### [79] [Liquid Neural Network Models for Natural Gas Spot Price Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24788)
*Yiqian Liu,Jiayi Niu,Adam Kelleher,Subhabrata Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用液体神经网络（LNNs）对Henry Hub天然气现货价格进行短期预测，以应对天然气价格的高波动性和非平稳性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然气是全球能源系统的重要组成部分，其价格受季节性需求、地缘政治发展和宏观经济条件变化的影响，表现出显著波动性。传统时间序列模型在面对非线性动态和频繁的机制变化时效果受限，因此需要更有效的预测方法。

Method: 采用液体神经网络（Liquid Neural Networks, LNNs）对Henry Hub现货价格进行短期预测。LNNs通过动态内部状态更新，能够持续适应演化的时间模式，特别适合处理非平稳的价格行为。

Result: LNNs在波动市场条件下能够提高预测准确性，为能源交易和电力市场应用中的决策支持提供更好的基础。（注：该摘要未给出具体实验数据和对比结果）

Conclusion: LNNs在天然气价格短期预测中具有应用潜力，能够降低预测不确定性，增强能源交易和电力市场应用中的决策支持能力。

Abstract: Natural gas is undoubtedly an essential component of the global energy system. Accurate short-term forecasting of natural gas price is challenging due to pronounced volatility driven by seasonal demand patterns, geopolitical developments, and shifting macroeconomic conditions. The nonlinear dynamics and frequent regime changes can limit the effectiveness of traditional time-series models. In this study, we explore the use of Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs) for short-horizon forecasting of the Henry Hub spot price, a primary benchmark for pricing. LNNs are designed to adapt continuously to evolving temporal patterns through dynamic internal state updates, making them well suited for nonstationary price behavior. By improving forecast accuracy in volatile market conditions, this work aims to reduce uncertainty and enhance decision support across energy trading and power market applications.

</details>


### [80] [Architecture Determines Observability in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24801)
*Thomas Carmichael*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“可观测性”概念，指通过监控中间层激活来检测自回归Transformer的自信错误。发现该特性在特定架构配置下会崩塌，且崩塌现象在训练过程中涌现。架构选择直接影响模型监控的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer模型会产生过度自信的错误，而现有的激活监控方法能否捕捉这些错误取决于模型是否在内部保留了输出置信度未暴露的信号。研究者需要理解这种信号保留是否为通用属性，以及架构和训练配方如何影响其可检测性。

Method: 定义“可观测性”指标：在控制最大softmax置信度和激活范数后，从冻结的中间层激活中线性读取每个token决策质量的能力。使用线性探针在13个模型、6个模型家族上进行实验，分析架构配置（如层数、注意力头数）和训练过程对可观测性的影响，并在Pythia控制套件中进行系统消融研究。

Result: 发现可观测性并非Transformer的通用属性：(1) Pythia中24层16头配置在3.5倍参数差距和两种Pile变体下均崩塌至rho_partial~0.10，而其他六种配置保持在0.21-0.38的健康范围；(2) 崩塌在训练过程中涌现，早期检查点存在信号但被训练抹除；(3) Qwen 2.5和Llama在3B规模相差2.9倍，Mistral 7B保留可观测性而Llama 3.1 8B崩塌；(4) WikiText训练的观察器可零样本迁移至下游QA任务，在20%标记率下独占捕捉10.9-13.4%的错误。

Conclusion: 模型架构选择是监控决策：可观测性崩塌是特定架构配置的涌现现象，非线性探针或层级扫描无法恢复。这表明在部署LLM时需将监控可行性纳入架构选择考量，某些架构天生更适合激活监控。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers make confident errors, but activation monitoring can catch them only if the model preserves an internal signal that output confidence does not expose. This preservation is determined by architecture and training recipe. We define observability as the linear readability of per-token decision quality from frozen mid-layer activations after controlling for max-softmax confidence and activation norm. The correction is essential. Confidence controls absorb 57.7% of raw probe signal on average across 13 models in 6 families.
  Observability is not a generic property of transformers. In Pythia's controlled suite, every tested run with the 24-layer, 16-head configuration collapses to rho_partial ~0.10 across a 3.5x parameter gap and two Pile variants, while six other configurations occupy a separated healthy band from 0.21 to 0.38. The output-controlled residual collapses at the same points, and neither tested nonlinear probes nor layer sweeps recover healthy-range signal. Checkpoint dynamics show the collapse is emergent during training. Both configurations at matched hidden dimension form the signal at the earliest measured checkpoint, but training erases it in the (24L, 16H) class while predictive loss continues improving.
  Across independent recipes the collapse map changes but the phenomenon persists. Qwen 2.5 and Llama differ by 2.9x at matched 3B scale with probe seed distributions that do not overlap, while Mistral 7B preserves observability where Llama 3.1 8B collapses despite similar broad architecture. A WikiText-trained observer transfers to downstream QA without training on those tasks, catching errors confidence misses. At 20% flag rate, its exclusive catch rate is 10.9-13.4% of all errors in seven of nine model-task cells. Architecture selection is a monitoring decision.

</details>


### [81] [PolyKV: A Shared Asymmetrically-Compressed KV Cache Pool for Multi-Agent LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24971)
*Ishan Patel,Ishan Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyKV提出了一个多智能体共享压缩KV缓存池系统，通过非对称压缩（Keys int8量化，Values 3-bit量化）实现97.7%的内存降低，同时仅带来0.57%的困惑度损失，有效解决了多智能体并发推理中的内存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型多智能体并发推理系统中，每个智能体通常需要分配独立的KV缓存，导致显存占用随智能体数量线性增长，严重限制了并发规模。为了突破这一内存瓶颈，本文探索是否可以让多个推理智能体共享单一压缩KV缓存池，从而大幅降低内存开销。

Method: PolyKV采用非对称压缩策略：Keys使用int8 (q8_0) 量化以保持softmax稳定性；Values使用TurboQuant MSE压缩，包括快速Walsh-Hadamard变换旋转和针对N(0,1)分布优化的3-bit Lloyd-Max量化。系统通过HuggingFace DynamicCache对象将压缩后的缓存注入到N个独立的智能体上下文中，实现一次写入、多读取的共享机制。

Result: 在SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct和Llama-3-8B-Instruct两种模型规模、三种上下文长度（600-7,194 tokens）以及最多15个并发智能体的配置下测试，PolyKV实现稳定的2.91倍压缩比。在Llama-3-8B上15个智能体共享4K token上下文时，KV缓存从19.8GB降至0.45GB（降低97.7%），困惑度仅增加0.57%，BERTScore F1均值达0.928。困惑度损失不随智能体数量增长，且随上下文长度增加而改善，在1,851个连贯tokens时甚至转为-0.26%。

Conclusion: PolyKV首次实现了多智能体共享单一有损压缩KV池的并发访问机制，在大幅降低内存占用的同时保持了模型性能。该方法证明了非对称压缩策略在多智能体场景下的有效性，为大语言模型多智能体系统的规模化部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We present PolyKV, a system in which multiple concurrent inference agents share a single, asymmetrically compressed KV cache pool. Rather than allocating a separate KV cache per agent -- the standard paradigm -- PolyKV writes a compressed cache once and injects it into N independent agent contexts via HuggingFace DynamicCache objects. Compression is asymmetric: Keys are quantized at int8 (q8_0) to preserve softmax stability, while Values are compressed using TurboQuant MSE -- a Fast Walsh-Hadamard Transform (FWHT) rotation followed by 3-bit Lloyd-Max quantization with centroids tuned to N(0,1). We evaluate across two model scales (SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct and Llama-3-8B-Instruct), three context lengths (600-7,194 tokens), and up to 15 concurrent agents. PolyKV achieves a stable 2.91x compression ratio across all configurations. On Llama-3-8B with 15 agents sharing a 4K-token context, PolyKV reduces KV cache memory from 19.8 GB to 0.45 GB -- a 97.7% reduction -- while maintaining only +0.57% perplexity degradation and a mean BERTScore F1 of 0.928. PPL delta does not grow with agent count and improves as context length increases, inverting to -0.26% at 1,851 coherent tokens. To our knowledge, no prior work combines a single shared, lossy-compressed KV pool with multi-reader concurrent agent access.

</details>


### [82] [Query-Efficient Quantum Approximate Optimization via Graph-Conditioned Trust Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24803)
*Molena Huynh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的条件信任域方法，用于降低低深度QAOA算法的查询成本（电路评估次数），将平均评估次数从基线的343和85次降至45次，同时保持相当的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低深度量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的实现中，主要计算成本往往来源于目标函数评估次数而非电路深度。现有的方法通常只提供初始参数估计，缺乏对搜索过程的有效引导，导致需要大量电路评估。因此需要一种能够利用图结构信息、基于预测不确定性动态调整搜索策略的方法，以减少查询成本。

Method: 提出图条件信任域方法：1) 使用图神经网络预测QAOA参数的高斯分布N(μ, Σ)；2) 均值μ作为局部优化器的初始化；3) 协方差Σ定义椭圆信任域约束搜索空间；4) 预测的不确定性确定实例相关的评估预算。该方法将学习到的分布定义为搜索策略，而不仅仅是初始参数估计。在明确的局部平滑性、曲率、校准和噪声假设下，推导了信任域内目标退化边界、梯度方差下界、去极化噪声下期望目标排序保持以及有限样本覆盖保证的理论界限。

Result: 在p=2深度的MaxCut问题上，针对Erdos-Renyi、3-regular、Barabasi-Albert和Watts-Strogatz图（n=8-16顶点）进行评估。相比随机重启和最强学习点预测基线，方法将平均电路评估次数从343和85次降至45±7次，同时保持采样近似比在基于浓度的启发式方法3个百分点范围内。预测不确定性校准良好（ECE=0.052，Spearman相关系数ρ=0.770），学习到的信任域可迁移至训练时未见过的图规模。方法未提升绝对近似比，优势在于降低查询成本。

Conclusion: 该研究识别出一个低深度、查询主导的QAOA运行机制，在此机制下图条件信任域能够在不修改变分电路结构的前提下显著降低查询成本。方法的关键创新在于将学习到的分布转化为搜索策略而非仅提供初始估计，并提供了理论保证。实验表明该方法在保持解质量的同时大幅减少电路评估次数，为低深度QAOA的实际应用提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In low-depth implementations of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), the dominant cost is often the number of objective evaluations rather than circuit depth. We introduce a graph-conditioned trust-region method for reducing this query cost. A graph neural network predicts a Gaussian distribution N(mu, Sigma) over QAOA angles. The mean initializes a local optimizer, the covariance defines an ellipsoidal trust region that constrains the search, and the predicted uncertainty determines an instance-dependent evaluation budget. Thus the learned distribution defines a search policy rather than only an initial parameter estimate.
  Under explicit assumptions on local smoothness, curvature, calibration, and noise, we derive bounds on objective degradation within the trust region, lower bounds on gradient variance, preservation of expected objective ordering under depolarizing noise, and finite-sample coverage guarantees. We evaluate the method for MaxCut at depth p = 2 on Erdos-Renyi, 3-regular, Barabasi-Albert, and Watts-Strogatz graphs with n = 8-16 vertices. Relative to random restarts and the strongest learned point-prediction baseline, the method reduces the mean number of circuit evaluations from 343 and 85 to 45 +/- 7, while maintaining sampled approximation ratios within 3 percentage points of concentration-based heuristics.
  The method does not improve absolute approximation ratios; its advantage is reduced query cost at comparable solution quality. The predictive uncertainty is calibrated in the experiments, with ECE = 0.052 and Spearman correlation rho = 0.770, and the learned trust regions transfer to graph sizes not used during training. The results identify a low-depth, query-dominated regime in which graph-conditioned trust regions reduce the query cost of QAOA without modifying the ansatz.

</details>


### [83] [Intrinsic Mutual Information as a Modulator for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24804)
*Peng Liao,Peijia Zheng,Lingbo Li,Shangsong Liang,Lin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RMiPO框架，通过利用响应级互信息进行偏好优化和超参数调制，在几乎不增加计算成本的情况下解决了离线偏好优化中的超参数调优问题，性能优于现有方法且训练开销降低超过15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线偏好优化方法（如DPO）虽然能有效对齐大语言模型与人类价值观，但通常需要额外的超参数调优才能达到最优性能，这导致了大量时间开销。尽管先前工作提出了一些改进方法，但这些方法在有效性上仍有限，且未能完全消除对超参数调优的依赖。

Method: 提出了RMiPO（Response-level Mutual information for Preference Optimization）框架，这是一个轻量级且高效的离线偏好优化方法。该框架利用内在的响应级互信息进行偏好优化，通过超参数调制动态解耦偏好贡献，且仅带来可忽略的额外计算成本。

Result: 广泛的实验结果表明，RMiPO在性能上持续优于现有方法，同时将训练开销减少了超过15%，证明了该方法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: RMiPO框架成功解决了离线偏好优化中的超参数调优问题，实现了轻量级、高效的偏好优化，为大语言模型与人类价值观的对齐提供了一种更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization methods, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), offer significant advantages in aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values. However, achieving optimal performance with these methods typically involves additional hyperparameter tuning, resulting in substantial time overhead. Although prior work has proposed a range of improvements, these methods remain limited in effectiveness and have not fully eliminated reliance on hyperparameter tuning. In this work, we propose RMiPO, a lightweight and efficient framework for offline preference optimization. RMiPO leverages intrinsic Response-level Mutual information for Preference Optimization with hyperparameter modulation, dynamically decoupling preference contributions at negligible additional computational cost. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that RMiPO achieves consistently superior performance over existing methods while reducing training overhead by more than 15\%. Our code is available at https://github.com/liavonpenn/rmipo.

</details>


### [84] [minAction.net: Energy-First Neural Architecture Design -- From Biological Principles to Systematic Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24805)
*Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出能量感知学习框架，通过大规模实验验证了能量约束的有效性。发现架构本身对准确率影响微弱，但架构与数据集的交互作用显著；通过能量正则化可将内部激活能量降至基线的6%而不损失准确率；能量优先架构带来5-33%的训练效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习在优化过程中未显式考虑内部计算成本，而物理和生物系统都在内在能量约束下运行。作者旨在探索如何在机器学习中引入能量感知机制，以弥合人工系统与自然系统在这一关键约束上的差异。

Method: 研究采用大规模实验设计，涵盖视觉、文本、神经形态和生理数据集，共2,203个实验，每个配置使用10个随机种子，进行因子统计分析。核心方法包括：1）架构与数据集交互效应的统计分析；2）四阶幅度lambda参数扫描验证单参数能量正则化目标函数L = L_CE + lambda * E(theta, x)；3）基于经典力学作用量泛函、统计物理自由能和变分推断KL正则化目标之间结构对应关系的能量优先架构设计。

Result: 三个核心发现：1）架构本身仅解释0.1%的准确率方差，而架构×数据集交互效应达44%（p < 0.001），证明最优架构严重依赖任务模态；2）在中等lambda值下，MNIST数据集上内部激活能量降至基线的6%，且无准确率损失；3）能量优先架构相比传统基线在模态内训练效率提升5-33%。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将学习过程解释为经典力学作用量泛函、统计物理自由能与变分推断KL正则化目标之间的结构对应关系，并将其框架化为设计假设而非推导。这为能量感知机器学习提供了理论基础和实证支持，挑战了通用最优架构的假设。

Abstract: Modern machine learning optimizes for accuracy without explicitly accounting for internal computational cost, even though physical and biological systems operate under intrinsic energy constraints. We evaluate energy-aware learning across 2,203 experiments spanning vision, text, neuromorphic, and physiological datasets, using 10 seeds per configuration and performing a factorial statistical analysis. Three findings emerge. First, architecture alone explains negligible variance in accuracy (partial eta^2 = 0.001). In contrast, the architecture x dataset interaction is large (partial eta^2 = 0.44, p < 0.001), demonstrating that optimal architecture depends critically on task modality and rejecting the assumption of a universal best architecture. Second, a controlled lambda-sweep over four orders of magnitude validates a single-parameter energy-regularized objective L = L_CE + lambda * E(theta, x): internal activation energy decreases to 6% of baseline at moderate lambda with no accuracy degradation on MNIST. Third, energy-first architectures inspired by an action-principle framework yield 5-33% within-modality training-efficiency gains over conventional baselines. These results emerge from a research program that interprets learning through a structural correspondence between the action functional in classical mechanics, free energy in statistical physics, and KL-regularized objectives in variational inference. We frame this correspondence as a design hypothesis rather than a derivation.

</details>


### [85] [Nautile-370M: Spectral Memory Meets Attention in a Small Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24809)
*Maixent Chenebaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Nautile-370M，一个3.71亿参数的高效推理小语言模型，采用混合架构结合SCA层与Transformer层，并证明SCA至少具有与完整自注意力同等的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对严格参数限制和推理预算约束下的高效推理需求，旨在融合结构化序列模型的长上下文效率与状态追踪优势，同时保留注意力机制的token间路由表达能力。

Method: 采用混合骨干架构，两层SeqCond Attention（SCA）层交替搭配一层Transformer层。SCA是受SeqCondenser启发的线性时间谱序列算子。训练在单Cloud TPU v4-64 pod切片上进行，后续强化学习阶段在单NVIDIA DGX Spark上完成。构建了专门的训练数据流水线，并设计了面向推理、验证和响应质量的强化学习阶段。

Result: 从理论上证明SCA读出机制能够从前缀摘要中精确检索任意单个token，并能将softmax注意力的任意输出作为特例复现，确立了SCA在连续极限下至少与完整自注意力具有同等表达能力。

Conclusion: Nautile-370M成功展示了在参数受限场景下通过混合架构实现高效推理的可行性，为小规模语言模型的架构设计提供了新的理论基础与实践方案。

Abstract: We present Nautile-370M, a 371-million-parameter small language model designed for efficient reasoning under strict parameter and inference budgets. Nautile-370M uses a hybrid backbone in which two SeqCond Attention (SCA) layers, a linear-time spectral sequence operator inspired by SeqCondenser, alternate with one transformer layer. This design aims to retain the long-context efficiency and state-tracking benefits of structured sequential models while preserving the expressive token-to-token routing of attention. The model was trained on a single Cloud TPU v4-64 pod slice provided through the Google TPU Research Cloud (TRC) program; the subsequent reinforcement learning stage was carried out on a single NVIDIA DGX Spark. We prove that the SCA readout mechanism can exactly retrieve any individual token from the prefix summary and can reproduce any output of softmax attention as a special case, establishing that SCA is at least as expressive as full self-attention in the continuous limit. We also describe the training data pipeline and outline a reinforcement learning stage specialized for reasoning, verification, and response quality.

</details>


### [86] [A Comparative Analysis on the Performance of Upper Confidence Bound Algorithms in Adaptive Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24810)
*Grigorios Papanikolaou,Ioannis Kontopoulos,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在自适应深度神经网络（ADNNs）中系统比较了五种上置信界（UCB）策略在边缘计算环境下的表现，发现UCB-Bayes收敛最快，而UCB-V和UCB-Tuned在精度-延迟和精度-能耗权衡中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算环境对能耗和延迟有严格限制，传统深度神经网络部署困难。尽管已有研究使用UCB1策略在ADNNs中动态选择置信度阈值以实现高效早退，但缺乏对其他UCB策略的系统研究与比较，因此需要探索更优的平衡策略。

Method: 在基于多臂老虎机（MAB）框架的自适应深度神经网络中，引入四种新的上置信界策略（UCB-V、UCB-Tuned、UCB-Bayes和UCB-BwK），并与传统的UCB1策略进行对比。在ResNet和MobileViT网络上，使用CIFAR-10、CIFAR-10.1和CIFAR-100数据集进行实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，所有策略均实现了次线性累积遗憾。其中，UCB-Bayes收敛速度最快，其次是UCB-Tuned和UCB-V。在精度-延迟和精度-能耗的帕累托前沿分析中，UCB-V和UCB-Tuned策略表现最优，实现了最佳的性能权衡。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统比较了多种UCB策略在边缘计算场景下的表现，证明了不同策略在收敛速度和性能权衡上各有优势，为ADNNs在资源受限环境中的部署提供了重要的策略选择依据。

Abstract: Edge computing environments impose strict constraints on energy consumption and latency, making the deployment of deep neural networks a significant challenge. Therefore, smart and adaptive inference strategies that dynamically balance computational cost or latency with predictive accuracy are critical in edge computing scenarios. In this work, we build on Adaptive Deep Neural Networks (ADNNs) that employ the Multi-Armed Bandit (MAB) framework. Current literature leverages the first version of the Upper Confidence Bound (UCB1) strategy to dynamically select the optimal confidence threshold, enabling efficient early exits without sacrificing accuracy. However, we introduce four additional Upper Confidence Bound strategies in ADNNs, namely UCB-V, UCB-Tuned, UCB-Bayes, and UCB-BwK, and perform, for the first time, a comparative study of these strategies with respect to trade-offs between accuracy, energy consumption, and latency. The proposed UCB strategies are employed on the ResNet and MobileViT neural networks, and are evaluated on the benchmark datasets of CIFAR-10, CIFAR-10.1, and CIFAR-100. Experimental results demonstrate that all strategies achieve sub-linear cumulative regret, with UCB-Bayes converging the fastest, followed by UCB-Tuned and UCB-V. Finally, UCB-V and UCB-Tuned dominate the Pareto Frontiers of accuracy-latency and accuracy-energy trade-offs.

</details>


### [87] [Time-varying Interaction Graph ODE for Dynamic Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24811)
*Xiaoyi Wang,Zhiqiang Wang,Jianqing Liang,Xingwang Zhao,Chuangyin Dang,Zhen Jin,Jiye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了时间变化交互图常微分方程（TI-ODE），通过可学习的交互基函数和时变权重的动态组合，解决了现有图神经ODE在动态图场景中难以捕获节点间交互模式多样性和时变性的问题，在多个数据集上实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图神经常微分方程（ODE）在动态图场景中通常采用统一的消息传递机制，假设节点间交互在任何时刻共享相同的传递函数，这导致难以捕获节点间交互模式的多样性和时变特性。

Method: 提出TI-ODE方法，其核心是将图ODE的演化函数分解为一组可学习的交互基函数，每个基函数对应一种不同类型的节点间交互。通过依赖时间的可学习权重动态组合这些基函数，使节点间交互模式能够随时间自适应演化。

Result: 在六个动态图数据集上的实验表明，TI-ODE在属性预测任务上始终优于现有方法并达到最先进性能；在Covid数据集上的实验验证了模型的可解释性和泛化能力；理论和实验均证明TI-ODE比使用统一消息传递机制的模型具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TI-ODE成功解决了动态图中节点交互模式多样性和时变性的建模挑战，通过基函数分解和时变权重组合实现了自适应的交互模式演化，在性能、可解释性和鲁棒性方面均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Graph neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) combine neural ODE with the message passing mechanism of Graph Neural Networks (GNN), providing a continuous-time modeling method for graph representation learning. However, in dynamic graph scenarios, existing graph neural ODEs typically employ a unified message passing mechanism, assuming that inter-node interactions share the same message passing function at any time, which makes it challenging to capture the diversity and time-varying nature of inter-node interaction patterns. To address this, we propose Time-varying Interaction Graph Ordinary Differential Equations (TI-ODE). The core idea of TI-ODE is to decompose the evolution function of a graph ODE into a set of learnable interaction basis functions, where each basis function corresponds to a distinct type of inter-node interaction. These basis functions are dynamically combined through time-dependent learnable weights, enabling inter-node interaction patterns to adaptively evolve over time. Experimental results on six dynamic graph datasets demonstrate that TI-ODE consistently outperforms existing methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance on attribute prediction tasks, and experiments on the \textit{Covid} dataset further verify the interpretability and generalizability of our TI-ODE. Furthermore, we demonstrate both theoretically and empirically that TI-ODE exhibits superior robustness compared to models utilizing a unified message-passing mechanism.

</details>


### [88] [Heterogeneous Variational Inference for Markov Degradation Hazard Models: Discretized Mixture with Interpretable Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24818)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合8状态离散化、30维特征工程、可解释性模型选择规则和ADVI推断方法的贝叶斯有限混合模型框架，在工业水泵设备数据上实现84倍加速且解决收敛不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯有限混合模型在识别离散风险集群时面临三个关键瓶颈：(1)粗粒度状态离散化导致退化信号不足；(2)当数据本身支持较少集群时集群识别不稳定；(3)MCMC方法在生产部署中计算不可行（每个模型需7+小时）。

Method: 提出四部分框架：(1)8状态全局百分位离散化增强退化事件；(2)30维特征工程（22个统计趋势特征+连续健康指标+PCA压缩至3维的文本嵌入）；(3)结合WAIC的可解释模型选择规则（强制最小集群份额和分离度）；(4)全秩协方差自动微分变分推断(ADVI)实现稳定快速估计。

Result: 在280台工业水泵设备的104,703条检测记录上验证：(1)随机效应模型基准显示ADVI与NUTS估计几乎相同但加速15倍；(2)有限混合模型在可解释性约束下识别最优集群数；(3)NUTS出现严重收敛问题和标签切换，而ADVI在时间减少84倍条件下提供稳定结果。

Conclusion: 贡献点包括：(1)首次证明细粒度状态离散化(8状态)对生存分析混合模型稳定性的关键作用；(2)融合统计、连续和语义信号的综合特征工程策略；(3)防止自动化模型选择过拟合的实用可解释性规则；(4)ADVI在收敛性、稳定性和计算效率方面优于NUTS的经验证据。

Abstract: Bayesian finite mixture models can identify discrete risk clusters (low-risk vs. high-risk equipment), but face three critical bottlenecks: (1) insufficient degradation signals from coarse state discretization, (2) unstable cluster identification when data inherently supports fewer clusters than explored, and (3) computational infeasibility of Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods for production deployment (7+ hours per model). We propose a practical framework combining (1) 8-state global percentile discretization that amplifies degradation events, (2) 30-dimensional feature engineering integrating statistical trends (22 features), continuous health indicators, and text embeddings (PCA-compressed to 3 dimensions), (3) interpretable model selection rules enforcing minimum cluster share and separation alongside WAIC, and (4) Automatic Differentiation Variational Inference (ADVI) with full-rank covariance for stable, fast estimation. Applied to 280 industrial pump equipment with 104,703 inspection records, we demonstrate: (1) Random effect models (baseline) show ADVI and NUTS produce nearly identical estimates with 15$\times$ speedup, validating ADVI accuracy. (2) Finite mixture models identify optimal number of clusters with interpretability constraints. (3) NUTS exhibits severe convergence issues and label switching, while ADVI provides stable results in 84$\times$ less time. We contributed that (1) First demonstration that fine-grained state discretization (8-state) is essential for mixture model stability in survival analysis.(2) Comprehensive feature engineering strategy combining statistical, continuous, and semantic signals. (3) Practical interpretability rules preventing overfitting in automated model selection. (4) Empirical evidence that ADVI outperforms NUTS for finite mixture models in terms of convergence, stability, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [89] [Negative Ontology of True Target for Machine Learning: Towards Evaluation and Learning under Democratic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24824)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的机器学习监督范式——民主监督，基于“真实目标不存在”的否定本体论假设，通过构建MIATTs（多重不准确真实目标）实现了评估与学习框架EL-MIATTs，并在实际教育场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流机器学习范式普遍假设存在一个客观真实的真实目标（TT），但这一假设在复杂现实场景中可能不成立。作者从哲学视角出发，质疑TT的客观存在性，旨在探索放弃该假设后如何构建新的机器学习评估与学习体系。

Method: 明确提出否定本体论视角，假设真实目标在现实世界中客观不存在；在此基础上定义民主监督；提出多重不准确真实目标（MIATTs）作为民主监督的实例级实现；构建包含逻辑驱动的MIATTs生成与评估原则、逻辑评估公式及不可定义真实目标学习的EL-MIATTs框架。

Result: 成功建立了EL-MIATTs理论框架，并在真实世界的教育个人发展应用中验证了该框架的潜力，支持了教育与专业发展领域的民主监督理念。

Conclusion: 通过放弃真实目标客观存在的假设，民主监督及其MIATTs实现为机器学习预测建模提供了新视角，推动了评估与学习范式的革新。

Abstract: This article philosophically examines how shifts in assumptions regarding the existence and non-existence of the true target (TT) give rise to new perspectives and insights for machine learning (ML)-based predictive modeling and, correspondingly, proposes a knowledge system for evaluation and learning under Democratic Supervision. By systematically analysing the existence assumption of the TT in current mainstream ML paradigms, we explicitly adopt a negative ontology perspective, positing that the TT does not objectively exist in the real world, and, grounded in this non-existence assumption, define Democratic Supervision for ML. We further present Multiple Inaccurate True Targets (MIATTs) as an instance-level realization of Democratic Supervision. Building upon MIATTs, we derive principles, for the logic-driven generation and assessment of MIATTs, a logical assessment formulation for evaluation with MIATTs, and undefinable true target learning for learning with MIATTs. Based on these components, we establish the evaluation and learning with MIATTs (EL-MIATTs) framework for ML-based predictive modelling. A real-world application demonstrates the potential of the proposed EL-MIATTs framework in supporting education and professional development for individuals, aligning with prior discussions of Democratic Supervision in the fields of education and professional development.

</details>


### [90] [Incompressible Knowledge Probes: Estimating Black-Box LLM Parameter Counts via Factual Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24827)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了不可压缩知识探针（IKP），通过测量模型的知识容量来推断闭源模型的参数规模，发现事实性知识容量随参数规模呈对数线性增长，并未出现饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源前沿模型实验室不披露参数数量，而传统的推理成本估算方法存在2倍以上的不确定性，需要一种更准确的替代方法来估算模型的实际参数规模。

Method: 构建包含1400个事实性问题的IKP基准（跨7个晦涩层级），在89个开放权重模型上校准准确率到参数数量的对数线性映射，并应用于188个模型的评估。

Result: 在开放模型上达到R²=0.917的预测精度，交叉验证中87.6%的预测在3倍误差内；发现MoE模型的总参数数（R²=0.79）比活跃参数数（R²=0.51）更能预测知识容量；知识容量随参数规模持续对数线性增长，未出现饱和。

Conclusion: 事实性知识容量持续随参数规模对数线性增长，所谓推理基准饱和并不意味着缩放的终结；程序性能力可以压缩，但事实性知识容量不能被架构改进压缩。

Abstract: Closed-source frontier labs do not disclose parameter counts, and the standard alternative -- inference economics -- carries $2\times$+ uncertainty from hardware, batching, and serving-stack assumptions external to the model. We exploit a tighter intrinsic bound: storing $F$ facts requires at least $F/$(bits per parameter) weights, so measuring how much a model \emph{knows} lower-bounds how many parameters it \emph{has}. We introduce \textbf{Incompressible Knowledge Probes (IKPs)}, a benchmark of 1{,}400 factual questions spanning 7 tiers of obscurity, designed to isolate knowledge that cannot be derived by reasoning or compressed by architectural improvements.
  We calibrate a log-linear mapping from IKP accuracy to parameter count on 89 open-weight models (135M--1,600B) spanning 19 vendors, achieving $R^2 = 0.917$; leave-one-out cross-validation confirms generalization (median fold error $1.59\times$, $68.5\%$ within $2\times$ and $87.6\%$ within $3\times$). For Mixture-of-Experts models, total parameters predict knowledge ($R^2 = 0.79$) far better than active parameters ($R^2 = 0.51$). We evaluate 188 models from 27 vendors and estimate effective knowledge capacity for all major proprietary frontier models; for heavily safety-tuned models the estimates are lower bounds, since refusal policy can hide tens of percentage points of "refused but known" capacity.
  The widely-reported saturation of reasoning benchmarks does not imply the end of scaling. Procedural capability compresses under the "Densing Law," but across 96 dated open-weight models the IKP time coefficient is $-0.0010$/month (95\% CI $[-0.0031, +0.0008]$) -- indistinguishable from zero, and rejecting the Densing prediction of $+0.0117$/month at $p < 10^{-15}$. Factual capacity continues to scale log-linearly with parameters across generations and across vendors.

</details>


### [91] [On the Trainability of Masked Diffusion Language Models via Blockwise Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24832)
*Yuxiang Wang,Yu Xiang,Baojian Zhou,Qifang Zhao,Keyue Jiang,Yanghua Xiao,Xiaoxiao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了掩码扩散语言模型（MDMs）的训练不稳定性问题，并提出了两种结合局部性感知的模块化模型（Jigsaw和Scatter），在保持扩散模型优势的同时提升了训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDMs）作为自回归大语言模型（AR-LLMs）的替代方案展现出潜力，但其优化过程存在显著的训练不稳定性问题，需要深入研究并解决这一局限性。

Method: 在三个受控任务（上下文线性回归、图路径查找、数独求解）上对比研究模块化MDMs与AR-LLMs的性能表现；提出两种局部性感知模块化模型：Jigsaw（在模块内强制自回归局部性）和Scatter（保持模块级迭代细化），通过注入从左到右的归纳偏置来缓解不稳定性。

Result: 标准随机掩码MDMs在线性回归任务上学习失败，在图路径查找任务上训练动态方差高，但在数独任务上优于AR-LLMs；Jigsaw在线性回归上匹配AR-LLM稳定性且在数独上表现强劲，Scatter在路径查找上保留了扩散模型的规划优势。

Conclusion: 标准随机掩码MDMs（包括模块化变体）对于有序生成任务可能是次优的扩散语言模型实例，这激励了超越随机掩码的模型设计，表明需要在保持扩散优势的同时引入适当的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to standard autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs), yet their optimization can be substantially less stable. We study blockwise MDMs and compare them with AR-LLMs on three controlled tasks that stress different aspects of structured generation: in-context linear regression, graph path-finding, and Sudoku solving. We find that standard random-masking MDMs fail to reliably learn linear regression, exhibit high variance training dynamics on graph path-finding, while outperforming AR-LLMs on Sudoku. To mitigate these instabilities, we propose two locality aware blockwise models, namely Jigsaw and Scatter, that inject left-to-right inductive bias by enforcing autoregressive locality within blocks while preserving iterative refinement at the block level. Empirically, Jigsaw matches AR-LLM stability on linear regression and remains strong on Sudoku, while Scatter retains diffusion's planning advantage on path-finding. Our results indicate that standard random-masking MDMs, even with blockwise variants, may be a suboptimal instantiation of diffusion LMs for ordered generation, motivating models beyond random masking.

</details>


### [92] [Transformer Approximations from ReLUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24878)
*Jerry Yao-Chieh Hu,Mingcheng Lu,Yi-Chen Lee,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统方法，将ReLU近似结果转换为softmax注意力机制，并在乘法、倒数和极值计算上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏将现有ReLU网络的近似理论迁移到softmax注意力机制的系统方法，且通用近似定理往往无法提供具体目标的资源界限。

Method: 提出了一个系统的转换配方，将ReLU近似结果系统地转化为softmax注意力机制的近似结果，覆盖多种常见近似目标。

Result: 成功在乘法、倒数计算和最小/最大值原语上展示了该配方的应用，获得了针对特定目标的经济资源界限，超越了通用近似声明。

Conclusion: 该工作为分析softmax transformer模型提供了新的分析工具，填补了ReLU近似理论与softmax注意力机制之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: We provide a systematic recipe for translating ReLU approximation results to softmax attention mechanism. This recipe covers many common approximation targets. Importantly, it yields target-specific, economic resource bounds beyond universal approximation statements. We showcase the recipe on multiplication, reciprocal computation, and min/max primitives. These results provide new analytical tools for analyzing softmax transformer models.

</details>


### [93] [Contrastive Image-Metadata Pre-Training for Materials Transmission Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24909)
*Georgia Channing,Debora Keller,Marta D. Rossell,Philip Torr,Rolf Erni,Stig Helveg,Henrik Eliasson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用大量未发表的TEM数据，构建了一个包含7,330张HAADF-STEM图像的数据集，通过学习图像与元数据的联合嵌入空间，训练了一个生成式风格迁移网络，能够将实验图像转换为不同仪器参数下的成像风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量透射电子显微镜(TEM)数据从未被发表，最终被删除。这些剩余数据包含丰富的细节和变化，且通常附带自动保存的仪器状态和采集参数元数据。作者希望利用这些被忽视的数据资源，挖掘图像风格与采集参数之间的关联。

Method: 作者收集了来自单一仪器的7,330张高角环形暗场扫描透射电镜(HAADF-STEM)图像及其元数据，学习图像与元数据之间的联合嵌入空间，建立图像风格与采集参数的联系，并基于此训练了一个生成式风格迁移网络。

Result: 该网络能够将实验图像转换为不同仪器参数记录下的风格，实现了基于物理参数的图像风格迁移。作者对网络性能进行了评估，并探索了该技术在物理去噪方面的应用价值。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了利用未发表TEM数据集学习图像与元数据联合嵌入的可行性，所开发的生成式风格迁移网络为物理去噪和图像处理提供了新的技术手段，展示了被忽视数据的潜在价值。

Abstract: The vast majority of transmission electron microscopy (TEM) data never gets published and ends up on a backup drive until deleted to free up space. These left-over datasets are rich in detail and variation, often paired with automatically saved metadata of instrument state and acquisition parameters. In this work, we introduce a dataset of 7,330 high-angle annular dark-field scanning-TEM (HAADF-STEM) images from a single instrument to learn a joint embedding space between image metadata and HAADF image. These embeddings link image style with acquisition parameters, which allows us to train a generative style transfer network that can convert experimental images into the style they would have had if they were recorded with different instrument parameters. We evaluate the performance of the network and explore the usefulness of the technique for physical denoising.

</details>


### [94] [Learning with Embedded Linear Equality Constraints via Variational Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24911)
*Matthew Marsh,Benoît Chachuat,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种贝叶斯框架，将线性物理约束嵌入机器学习过程，在电池模型学习中实现了更小的置信区间和更少的约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法在科学工程应用中缺乏有意义的不确定性估计，且预测结果可能违反已知的物理知识。

Method: 提出贝叶斯框架，将输入输出间的线性关系嵌入学习过程，同时对模型参数和领域知识进行完整的不确定性表征。

Result: 在单粒子电池模型（受电压和能量平衡约束）的实验中，相比基于变分推断的标准贝叶斯神经网络，该方法提供了更小的可信区间和更少的约束违反。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效融合物理知识约束与不确定性量化，在保持预测准确性的同时提升了模型在科学工程应用中的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Machine Learning is becoming more prevalent in science and engineering, but many approaches do not provide meaningful uncertainty estimates and predictions may also violate known physical knowledge. We propose a Bayesian framework to embed linear relationships across inputs and outputs into the learning process, whilst characterizing full predictive uncertainty over both the model parameters and the domain knowledge. We evaluated our method on learning the single particle battery model subject to voltage and energy balances, showing its ability to provide reduced credible intervals and constraint violations compared to standard Bayesian neural networks based on variational inference.

</details>


### [95] [Generative diffusion models for spatiotemporal influenza forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24913)
*Joseph Lemaitre,Justin Lessler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Influpaint，一种基于去噪扩散概率模型的流感预测方法，将流行病预测转化为图像修复任务，在CDC实时预测挑战中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机制模型和统计方法难以捕捉流行病预测中的多模态不确定性和突发趋势，需要能够更好建模复杂时空动态的预测框架。

Method: 将流感季节编码为时空图像（像素强度代表发病率），采用去噪扩散概率模型从混合数据集（监测数据和模拟轨迹）学习疾病动态分布，将预测任务转化为条件生成（图像修复）任务。

Result: Influpaint生成真实、多样的流行病轨迹，回溯评估中预测精度与领先集成方法相当；在2023-2025年CDC FluSight实时挑战中表现持续提升，最佳性能来自30%监测数据+70%模拟轨迹的训练组合。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能有效捕捉流感动态的重要时空结构，为概率性传染病预测提供了灵活框架。

Abstract: Forecasting infectious disease incidence can provide important information to guide public health planning, yet is difficult because epidemic dynamics are complex. Current mechanistic and statistical approaches often struggle to capture multimodal uncertainty or emergent trends. Influpaint adapts denoising diffusion probabilistic models to epidemic forecasting. By encoding influenza seasons as spatiotemporal images in which pixel intensity represents incidence, Influpaint learns a rich distribution of disease dynamics from a hybrid dataset of surveillance and simulated trajectories. Forecasting is formulated as a conditional generation (inpainting) task from partial observations. We show that Influpaint generates realistic, diverse epidemic trajectories and achieves forecast accuracy that is competitive with leading ensemble methods in retrospective evaluation. In real-time evaluation during the 2023--2025 U.S. CDC FluSight challenges, performance improved substantially across seasons, with highly accurate but somewhat overconfident projections in 2024--2025. The best performance was achieved with a training dataset containing 30% surveillance and 70% simulated trajectories. These results show that diffusion models can capture important spatiotemporal structure in influenza dynamics and provide a flexible framework for probabilistic infectious disease forecasting.

</details>


### [96] [A Unifying Framework for Unsupervised Concept Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24936)
*Chandler Squires,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无监督概念提取的统一理论框架，将概念提取任务建模为识别生成模型，并提供了一个通用的可识别性元定理，大大简化了证明保证的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念提取技术（如稀疏自编码器和变换器）在用于模型引导和遗忘等下游任务时，缺乏对其保证（或缺乏保证）的理解，因此需要建立一个统一的理论框架来分析和确立这些方法的可识别性保证。

Method: 将无监督概念提取任务框架化为识别生成模型的问题，提出了一个通用的可识别性元定理，该定理将建立可识别性保证的问题简化为刻画两个集合交集的问题。

Result: 通过在多种广泛使用的方法上演示，该元定理大大简化了证明此类保证的任务，证明了理论框架的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 这一统一理论框架为概念提取方法的可识别性分析提供了强有力的工具，为开发新的、有原则的概念提取方法铺平了道路。

Abstract: Techniques for concept extraction, such as sparse autoencoders and transcoders, aim to extract high-level symbolic concepts from low-level nonsymbolic representations. When these extracted concepts are used for downstream tasks such as model steering and unlearning, it is essential to understand their guarantees, or lack thereof. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for unsupervised concept extraction, in which we frame the task of concept extraction as identifying a generative model. We present a general meta-theorem for identifiability, which reduces the problem of establishing identifiability guarantees to the problem of characterizing the intersection of two sets. As we demonstrate on a range of widely-used approaches, this meta-theorem substantially simplifies the task of proving such guarantees, thus paving the way for the development of new, principled approaches for concept extraction.

</details>


### [97] [Rethinking Layer Redundancy in Large Language Models: Calibration Objectives and Search for Depth Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24938)
*Minkyu Kim,Vincent-Daniel Yun,Youngrae Kim,Youngjin Heo,Suin Cho,Seong-hun Kim,Woosang Lim,Gaeul Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从功能性视角审视大语言模型的层冗余，发现校准目标比搜索算法对深度剪枝结果影响更大，不同目标产生的冗余层存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度剪枝工作主要关注重要性标准和搜索算法，将层冗余视为预训练网络的固有属性。本文旨在挑战这一假设，从功能性视角探究冗余是否受模型和评估目标的共同影响，以及通用排序是否足够。

Method: 在三个LLM系列、两个校准目标和七种搜索算法上进行了系统的实证研究，分析了不同校准目标下的冗余层差异，并比较了困惑度与下游任务准确率排序的一致性，同时评估了固定目标下不同搜索算法的表现。

Result: 研究发现：（1）不同校准目标会产生定性上不同的冗余层；（2）困惑度和下游任务准确率的排序并不总是一致；（3）在固定校准目标下，不同搜索算法倾向于产生相似的解决方案；（4）校准目标的选择比搜索算法对剪枝结果的影响更大。

Conclusion: 校准目标在深度剪枝中起关键作用，通用排序可能不足以表征层冗余。未来研究应更加重视校准目标的设计，而非仅仅优化搜索算法。

Abstract: Depth pruning improves the inference efficiency of large language models by removing Transformer blocks. Prior work has focused on importance criteria and search algorithms, often treating layer redundancy as an inherent structural property of pretrained networks. In contrast, we adopt a \emph{functional perspective}, where redundancy is jointly influenced by the model and the evaluation objective, suggesting that a universal ranking may not be sufficient. Through an empirical study across three LLM families, two calibration objectives, and seven search algorithms, we observe that different objectives yield qualitatively different redundant layers, and that perplexity and downstream accuracy rankings do not consistently align. Under a fixed objective, however, search algorithms tend to produce similar solutions. Overall, our results suggest that the calibration objective may play a more influential role than the choice of search algorithm, indicating that further attention to objective design could be beneficial.

</details>

Zelenfroynd,Meline Mkrtchyan,Qing Miao,Omri Almog,Bhavesh Pawar,Rameshwar Shivbhakta,Sudeep Sabnis,Ashrton Sharabiani,Negar Habibi,Geethapriya Venkataramani,Pamela Peng,Prerit Rodney,Serge Panev,Richard Mazzarese,Nicky Liu,Michael Fukuyama,Andrii Skliar,Roger Waleffe,Duncan Riach,Yunheng Zou,Jian Hu,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Yuhao Yang,Zuhair Ahmed,Alexandre Milesi,Carlo del Mundo,Chad Cui,Michael Evans,Katherine Luna,Jie Lou,Pinky Xu,Guyue Huang,Negar Habibi,Michael Boone,Pradeep Thalasta,Adeola Adesoba,Dina Yared,Christopher Parisien,Leon Derczynski,Shaona Ghosh,Wes Feely,Micah Schaffer,Radha Sri-Tharan,Jeffrey Glick,Barnaby Simkin,George Zelenfroynd,Tomasz Grzegorzek,Rishabh Garg,Aastha Jhunjhunwala,Sergei Kolchenko,Farzan Memarian,Haran Kumar,Shiv Kumar,Isabel Ginsburg,Jan Kautz,Eileen Long,Mohammad Shoeybi,Mostofa Patwary,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Andrew Tao,Bryan Catanzaro,Udi Karpas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Nemotron 3 Nano Omni，这是Nemotron多模态系列中首个原生支持音频、文本、图像和视频输入的模型。该模型在架构、训练数据和策略上进行了改进，实现了全面的性能提升和更高效的推理能力，并开源了模型权重和部分训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在多模态模型中实现对音频的本地原生支持，并在保持模型规模相近的情况下提升各模态的处理精度、降低推理延迟和提高吞吐量，以满足现实世界中文档理解、长音频视频理解以及智能体计算机使用等任务的需求。

Method: 该模型基于高效的Nemotron 3 Nano 30B-A3B骨干网络构建，创新性地引入了多模态token缩减技术，并结合先进的训练数据和配方进行训练。通过架构优化，模型能够同时处理文本、图像、视频和音频四种模态的输入。

Result: Nemotron 3 Nano Omni在所有模态上的准确率均超越其前身Nemotron Nano V2 VL，在真实文档理解、长音频视频理解以及智能体计算机使用等任务上取得了领先结果。同时，相比同规模的其他模型，该模型实现了显著更低的推理延迟和更高的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Nemotron 3 Nano Omni成功实现了对多模态（包括音频）的高效处理，在性能和效率上均有显著提升。研究团队发布了BF16、FP8和FP4格式的模型权重、部分训练数据和代码库，以促进相关研究和应用的发展。

Abstract: We introduce Nemotron 3 Nano Omni, the latest model in the Nemotron multimodal series and the first to natively support audio inputs alongside text, images, and video. Nemotron 3 Nano Omni delivers consistent accuracy improvements over its predecessor, Nemotron Nano V2 VL, across all modalities, enabled by advances in architecture, training data and recipes. In particular, Nemotron 3 delivers leading results in real-world document understanding, long audio-video comprehension, and agentic computer use. Built on the highly efficient Nemotron 3 Nano 30B-A3B backbone, Nemotron 3 Nano Omni further incorporates innovative multimodal token-reduction techniques to deliver substantially lower inference latency and higher throughput than other models of similar size. We are releasing model checkpoints in BF16, FP8, and FP4 formats, along with portions of the training data and codebase to facilitate further research and development.

</details>


### [99] [Compute Aligned Training: Optimizing for Test Time Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24957)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“计算对齐训练”（Compute Aligned Training），通过推导新的损失函数将训练目标与测试时策略对齐，从而显著提升了大语言模型在测试时的计算扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的训练范式（如监督微调SFT和强化学习RL）优化的是基础策略下单个样本的似然性，这与依赖聚合或过滤输出的测试时计算策略（如Best-of-N）存在目标错位，限制了模型在测试时的性能提升。

Method: 提出计算对齐训练框架，将推理策略概念化为对基础策略的算子，以此推导出专门的新损失函数。这些函数针对SFT和RL进行了实例化，旨在当应用测试时策略（如采样、聚合等）时最大化模型性能。

Result: 实验证明，与标准训练方法相比，该方法显著提升了测试时计算扩展的效果，验证了训练与推理目标对齐的有效性。

Conclusion: 将训练目标与测试时推理策略显式对齐是提升大语言模型性能的有效途径，为优化训练范式提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has emerged as a powerful mechanism for enhancing Large Language Model (LLM) performance. However, standard post-training paradigms, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL), optimize the likelihood of individual samples under a base policy, creating a misalignment with test time procedures that rely on aggregated or filtered outputs. In this work, we propose Compute Aligned Training, which aligns training objectives with test-time strategies. By conceptualizing inference strategies as operators on the base policy, we derive new loss functions that maximize performance when said strategies are applied. We instantiate such loss functions for SFT and RL across common test time strategies. Finally, we provide empirical evidence that this training method substantially improves test time scaling over standard training.

</details>


### [100] [CoreFlow: Low-Rank Matrix Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24959)
*Dongze Wu,Linglingzhi Zhu,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoreFlow是一种几何保持的低秩流模型，通过学习矩阵分布共享的行/列子空间并在诱导的低维核心上训练连续归一化流，实现了高维有限样本场景下矩阵分布的高效学习与生成，同时支持不完整训练矩阵的处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维矩阵分布学习面临挑战：当矩阵维度大而样本量有限时，在环境空间进行生成建模计算成本高昂且统计脆弱，缺乏有效方法在保持矩阵结构的同时提升训练效率。

Method: 提出CoreFlow方法：(1)学习矩阵分布共享的低秩行/列子空间；(2)仅在诱导的低维核心上训练连续归一化流；(3)分离共享矩阵几何与样本特定变异；(4)通过掩码黎曼更新和迭代补全处理不完整训练矩阵。

Result: 在真实和合成基准测试中，CoreFlow在少样本场景下显著提升了谱和矩层面的生成质量，在数据丰富场景下保持竞争力；即使压缩至环境维度的9%且训练数据缺失高达40%，仍能有效工作。

Conclusion: CoreFlow成功解决了高维有限样本下矩阵分布学习的核心难题，通过几何保持的低秩分解实现了效率与质量的双重提升，并具备处理不完整数据的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning matrix-valued distributions from high-dimensional and possibly incomplete training data is challenging: ambient-space generative modeling is computationally expensive and statistically fragile when the matrix dimension is large but the sample size is limited.
